## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

This is a live keynote deck with a coherent black, gold and green identity, and strong hero slides: the CUDA-X map on 5, the superchip on 14 and the data center photos on 16 to 19 have clear subjects and a consistent label layout. The weaknesses are real for anyone reading it without the speaker. Slides 8 to 12 carry reasoning text so small and dim that it cannot be read. Slides 37 to 52 are dense partner architecture diagrams with tiny labels, and slides 13 and 15 are identical, as are the repeated Omniverse slides, so some pages add little. Many slides lack a statement of what the viewer should take away, and the slide 36 table is illegible.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent dark identity with gold titles, the single-subject hero imagery on slides 5, 14 and 16 to 19, and the 3-line site labels with power figures.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge or remove the unreadable reasoning text on slides 8 to 12 and the dim table on 36, and simplify the partner diagrams on 37 to 52 to one readable message each.

**What would change the score:** I would cut duplicate slides such as 13 and 15 and the near-identical build frames, and add short takeaway titles on the diagram and photo-only slides.

_Confidence medium. 72 of 72 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with the NVIDIA transformation from GPU chip maker to AI infrastructure provider and then moves through its main sections in order. These cover the CUDA-X library stack, 6G and AI-RAN signal processing, quantum-GPU computing, agentic AI and reasoning workloads, physical AI and robotics, and the Grace Blackwell NVL72 rack-scale systems. Partner data center projects from CoreWeave, Oracle, xAI and Stargate are shown with their power capacity and locations, followed by the Taiwan AI infrastructure announcement with Foxconn, TSMC and NVIDIA. Product sections cover NVLink Fusion for custom ASICs and CPUs, the DGX Station and RTX PRO Server, and the enterprise AI platform. Later slides focus on Isaac GR00T humanoid robot models and Omniverse digital twins, including a Kaohsiung City twin. The deck closes with a single-line summary slide. Slides worth studying include the CUDA-X library map on slide 5, the rack-scale hardware reveal on slide 14, and the partner data center slides on 16 to 19, where each site is labelled with its power figure in one consistent layout.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the NVIDIA presentation deck have and how is it structured?**

The NVIDIA deck has 72 slides. It opens with the transformation from GPU chip maker to AI infrastructure provider on slide 3, then moves through CUDA-X libraries, 6G and AI-RAN, quantum-GPU computing, agentic AI, physical AI and robotics, Grace Blackwell NVL72 systems, partner data centers, the Taiwan announcement, and product sections, and closes with a single-line summary slide.

**What power capacity does the NVIDIA deck list for the CoreWeave data center project?**

Slide 16 labels the CoreWeave project as 220 MW in the USA, with the text 'Scaling Globally'. The slide credits the image as courtesy of CoreWeave.

**What does the NVIDIA deck say about the Taiwan AI infrastructure announcement?**

Slide 21 is titled 'Announcing Foxconn, Taiwan, TSMC and NVIDIA Build AI Infrastructure for Taiwan'. It shows the logos of FOXCONN, HON HAI TECHNOLOGY GROUP, NSTC, tsmc and NVIDIA.

**What performance figures does NVIDIA give for the RTX PRO Server on slide 31?**

Slide 31 lists 30 PFLOPS FP4 AI Performance and 8 x RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition GPUs. It also lists 3 PFLOPS RTX Graphics Performance and 800 GB GPU Memory.

No footer, slide number or logo is visible; margins are about 8 to 9% on the left and 2 to 5% at the top and bottom.

**Visuals**

Hand-drawn, sketch-style line illustration with white outlines and gold-olive shading, shown as a chain of four stages connected by arrows. (1) CHIP, lower-left at about x 8.5%, y 83.7%, w 3.4%, h 3.5%: a small square GPU die drawn with thin white outlines and a glowing gold-olive centre. A horizontal white sketched line runs from the chip to the system (about x 13.5% to 25.5%, y about 85.6%), tapering at both ends. (2) SYSTEM, about x 27.2%, y 78.8%, w 10.1%, h 8.5%: a flat, wide server tray with rounded corners, white outline, ports and vents along its front, and gold-olive streak shading across its body. A sketched white arrow runs from the system's upper-right to the data center (about x 38.8% to 50%, y 79.5% to 68%). (3) DATA CENTER, about x 45.6%, y 53.8%, w 20.5%, h 12.5%: a long rectangular rack-like enclosure drawn as a white grid of vertical and horizontal lines with a central band of glowing gold-olive light. A sketched white arrow with a solid triangular head points up and right from the data center's upper-right corner (about x 59% to 67.5%, y 54% to 37%). (4) INFRASTRUCTURE, about x 54.2%, y 7.9%, w 45.8%, h 20.5%: a cluster of five or six flat, boxed server trays in an isometric arrangement, drawn in white outline with gold-olive streaks and a diagonal tilted panel running through the middle; the stack is arranged in three tiers in an oblique grid and the top-right tray extends to the slide edge with a faint gold smear. Labels are small uppercase, thin sans-serif, white, centred under each stage. Lighting is a soft glow of gold-olive highlights on black; no photographs, no cropping, no overlays other than the glow.

Background: Solid black #000000 full-slide image, with a white #FFFFFF fill beneath it fully covered. Title and subtitle are white (#FFFFFF), sans-serif (NVIDIASans-Medium, a grotesque with a single regular weight), title and subtitle both about 3.7% of slide height (cap height about 2.6%), set in one centred block with no bold. **Review notes**

- Worth studying: A dense library map is organised into labelled domain blocks, making a large software catalogue readable at a glance.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent dark identity with gold titles, the single-subject hero imagery on slides 5, 14 and 16 to 19, and the 3-line site labels with power figures.

**Text**

Accelerated Computing Begins With CUDA-X Libraries / Computational Lithography / cuLitho / cuDSS / cuSPARSE / cuFFT / AmgX / Computer Aided / Engineering / cuOpt / Decision / Optimization / Warp / Physics / cuDF / cuML / Data Science / andProcessing / Megatron / Dynamo / NIXL / cuDNN / CUTLASS / Deep / Learning / cuEquivariance / cuTensor / Quantum / Chemistry / cuQuantum / CUDA-Q / Quantum / Computing / Earth-2 / Weather / Analytics / MONAI / Medical / Imaging / Parabricks / Genomics / Aerial / Sionna / 5G/6G / Signal Processing / cuPyNumeric / Numerical / Computing

**Layout**

A single centred title sits in the upper third, horizontally centred at about 17% from the top, above a horizontal row of 13 tall rounded cards that spans nearly the full slide width from about 2% to 98%. Each card has a caption block beneath it: a gold product name or list, then a white descriptor, all centre-aligned under its card, with the captions stepping down in a slight diagonal that follows the perspective of the row. No footer, page number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Thirteen rounded-rectangle picture cards in one row, each slightly tilted and overlapping its neighbour, decreasing in height toward the right edge. 1 cuLitho: lithography machine render, dark blue-black with glowing cyan chip rectangles and a bright cyan base light. 2 cuDSS: silver-grey car body in a studio with yellow and teal particle simulation at the lower edge. 3 cuOpt: glowing green node at the centre of a blue wireframe network mesh. 4 Warp: swirling multicolour fluid simulation in magenta, green, blue and white on black. 5 cuDF: dark background with scattered purple and teal point-cloud speckles. 6 Megatron: blue network diagram with grey stacked boxes, connectors and small nodes. 7 cuEquivariance: ball-and-stick molecule with cyan, blue, red and white atoms on black. 8 cuQuantum: gold-brown dilution refrigerator, a cylindrical cryostat with tiers lit from below. 9 Earth-2: Earth from orbit with white clouds and coloured weather-pattern overlays over blue ocean. 10 MONAI: multicolour 3D segmented anatomy in green, pink, orange, white and red on black. 11 Parabricks: blue protein surface with an orange ribbon structure. 12 Aerial Sionna: aerial city view with grey rooftops and green radio ray-tracing lines. 13 cuPyNumeric: galaxy nebula in magenta, blue, orange and white on black. The cards are photographic or rendered, shown as boxed tiles with rounded corners and a thin beige outline.

Background: Solid black #000000 across the full slide.

### Slide 6

**Text**

NVIDIA Building 6G on AI
Aerial
Sionna
5G/6G
Signal Processing
NVIDIA Grace CPU
NVIDIA PCIe GPU
NVIDIA ConnectX-7
Compact AI-RAN

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with three zones. A phone-shaped aerial city image with its label sits at the left, a server hardware image with its caption sits in the centre-left, and a centred title with a 3-row logo grid sits at the right. The title is at the top right, the logos sit in three staggered rows below it, and there is no footer, page number or slide number.

**Visuals**

1) Photograph/illustration of an aerial city view in a tall rounded-rectangle phone frame (841x1205 px, boxed, rounded corners, thin tan-gold border) at x 7.1 y 22.4 w 21.9 h 55.8. Subject: a dense European-style city block seen from above, with grey and red-tile rooftops, tree-lined streets and a road at the bottom, overlaid with bright green ray-tracing lines radiating from a red point near the top and a yellow cylinder (transmitter/antenna marker) at the lower left. Composition is top-down, fully cropped to the frame. Lighting is daylight; dominant colours are grey, green, red and yellow. 2) Photograph of NVIDIA server hardware (1625x1097 px) at x 22.6 y 42.9 w 26.9 h 32.3, unboxed and partly overlapping the phone frame at its left edge. Subject: a 2U chassis with a dark grey metal case, a large exposed circuit board with a central processor package and heatsinks, a vertical PCIe GPU card with a black heatsink, and a small add-in card at upper right, with two drive-bay style ports at the lower right. Lighting is studio, shot at an angle; dominant colours are black, dark grey and metallic silver. 3) Logos in white on black: SoftBank, T-Mobile, indosat ooredoo hutchison, vodafone, NOKIA, SAMSUNG, KYOCERA, FUJITSU, cisco. Row 1: SoftBank, T-Mobile, indosat ooredoo hutchison, vodafone. Row 2: NOKIA, SAMSUNG, KYOCERA. Row 3: FUJITSU, cisco. 4) A small image at x 28.0 y 73.5 w 22.4 h 7.7 (898x173 px) that carries the NVIDIA Grace CPU / NVIDIA PCIe GPU / NVIDIA ConnectX-7 caption block; its text is rendered in the gold colour listed below.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed (a full-slide black image covers the white #FFFFFF base fill). Black background (#000000) with white and tan-gold type. Title is white NVIDIASans Medium, about 3.7% of slide height, sentence case. Body and caption text is NVIDIASans Regular or Medium at about 1.7 to 2.0% of slide height. Gold accent #C1B485 is used for the Aerial, Sionna and hardware caption lines. Sans-serif geometric typeface, no shadows or gradients. The phone frame has rounded corners with a thin tan-gold border. Logos are rendered in white.

**Layout**

Two zones on a black field: a left group of a tall GB200 NVL72 rack photo with a framed cryostat photo overlapping its left side, and captions beneath, plus a right block with the title at top right and six rows of logos below it. The title is centred over the right block at about y 18 to 26 percent. Logos sit in six staggered rows, roughly four to five per row, left-aligned to a common block from about x 51 to 98 percent. No footer, no slide number and no logo in the corners; the GB200 NVL72 label sits to the right of the rack base at about y 86 percent.

**Visuals**

Photograph 1: cryostat dilution refrigerator, framed in a rounded rectangle at x 7.4 y 22.9 w 22.2 h 55.3 percent. Subject: a quantum computer cryostat with gold and copper tiers and cabling hanging from a silver top flange, viewed from the front and slightly above; the tiers narrow toward the base where a cylindrical metal shield sits. Composition: portrait, centred, filling the frame; not cropped at the edges. Lighting: warm directional light on the gold and copper parts against a dark, nearly black backdrop. Dominant colours: amber #B8731F, copper #8A4B1E, warm gold #C9A24A, near-black #0A0A0A. Photo is boxed inside a rounded rectangle with a thin tan-gold stroke. Photograph 2: NVIDIA GB200 NVL72 server rack at x 22.3 y 7.6 w 13.8 h 86.0 percent, tall narrow and standing on a dark plinth; it partly sits behind the cryostat frame on its left edge. Subject: a dark cabinet with horizontal compute trays and two lit gold-edged blades, with cable bundles on the sides. Lighting: soft warm highlights on the trays. Dominant colours: charcoal #1E1E1E, black #0B0B0B, gold highlights #C1B485. Not cropped; boxed, no frame. Logos: 27 company logos in white (monochrome) as listed in the elements, in the right block; no coloured logos.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (full-slide image in black behind all elements). Black full-slide background with no gradient or pattern. Title in NVIDIASans Medium, white #FFFFFF, two lines centred, about 3.7 percent of slide height per line, sentence case. Caption cuQuantum and CUDA-Q in NVIDIASans Medium, gold #C1B485, about 2.0 percent of slide height. Caption Quantum Computing and GB200 NVL72 in NVIDIASans Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 1.7 percent of slide height. Logos in white monochrome, thin to medium weight, with generous spacing. Photo frame: rounded rectangle, corner radius about 3 percent of slide width, thin tan-gold #C1B485 stroke about 0.2 percent of slide width, no shadow. Accent colour is tan-gold #C1B485. No visible shadows.

**Review notes**

**Text**

Agentic AI / “Understand-Think-Act” is 100-1000X More Compute Than One-Shot / 你是我在台北的行程規劃師。 / 請從台北流行音樂中心出發，規劃一個三小時（18:30–21:30）的行程。捷運加步行。造訪台北五大夜市中的至少三個。品嚐六種不同的台灣特色街頭小吃，其中一種需來自米其林必比登或米其林入選的攤位。請列出詳細的時間表，包括抵達時間、攤位名稱、菜品、捷運路線與站名、步行距離、路線說明和每個資訊的出處。最後，請以約50個字摘要此行程的亮點。 / Reasoning / Okay, let's tackle this. The user needs three search queries to help plan the sections of their / Taiwan night market food tour report. The tour starts at Taipei Music Center, let’s say it takes / the MRT to Raohe, then Ningxia, and ends at Shilin by 21:30. Each stop has specific iconic foods. / The report sections probably break down each market visit, the foods, timing, etc. / The queries should help answer questions in the report's sections. Let's look at the plan's / components: locations (markets), specific foods, timing, transportation (MRT), and ensuring the

**Layout**

Three-column composition on a black field: a rounded-corner text panel on the left, a central glowing artwork stack (tall card above a box-shaped 'AI FACTORY' illustration) in the middle, and a rounded-corner image panel on the right holding a 3D avatar. The centred two-line title sits at the top, about 8% from the top edge. Left and right panels share a top edge at 36.1% and a height of about 31%; the right panel's 'Reasoning' label and small body text sit below it at left x 67%. Side margins are about 2.2% (left) and about 2% (right). There is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Left: a thin light-grey rounded-rectangle outline panel (x 2.2–33.6%, y 36.1–67.3%) containing three paragraphs of Chinese text, the first a single line and the second a wrapped block. Centre: a tall rounded-rectangle card (about x 44–57%, y 31.6–71.8%) with a brushed-ink texture in pale yellow-gold (#D9C77A) fading to near-black at the edges; two dark ink-brush Chinese characters sit on the card, and a glowing white-edged outline frames it. Flanking the card are two vertical stacks of five small glowing rectangular blocks (left stack x 41–44.8%, right stack x 55–59.7%, y about 37–65%), drawn in white outline with gold glow. Below the card is a sketched open box with an upper gold-brushed lid edge, labelled 'AI FACTORY' in white capital letters, outlined in white on black, with a faint gold glow (about x 39.3–60.4%, y 69.5–86.1%). Connecting arrows: two thin grey horizontal lines with small filled arrowheads, one from the left panel to the centre stack at y 51% (x 34.9–41.2%), and one from the centre to the right panel at y 51% (x 59.7–66%). Right: a rounded-rectangle panel (x 66.7–98%, y 36.1–67.3%) holding a 3D-rendered cartoon avatar of an older woman with short swept grey hair, thin dark-rimmed glasses, a slight smile and a dark grey leather jacket, cropped at the bottom edge of the panel, lit softly from the front on a black background. A faint unlabelled image strip sits at about x 66.7–98%, y 73.6–84.9%, showing nothing distinct against the black. Shapes and images are not otherwise decorated.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed background. Pure black background throughout. Title white (#FFFFFF) NVIDIA Sans Medium, sans-serif, regular weight, about 3.7% of slide height for both title lines, centred. Left panel body text white (#FFFFFF) HG Maru Gothic-style rounded Japanese sans, about 1.73% of slide height, line spacing about 2.1% of slide height. 'Reasoning' label in bronze-gold #A69060 NVIDIA Sans Regular, about 1.73%. Reasoning body text white NVIDIA Sans Regular, about 1.11%, fading to dark grey toward the bottom two lines. Panel outlines in light grey (#8C8C8C), about 0.2% of slide height stroke, corner radius about 2.5% of slide width. Connector arrows in grey #8C8C8C, thin 1 px-equivalent stroke. Central card has a gold-yellow (#D9C77A) brushed texture with soft glow; blocks and box carry white outlines with warm gold glow. No drop shadows, no gradient backgrounds; the glow is the only lighting effect.

Physical AI
The Foundation of Robotic Revolution

between packed cars, causing the ego driver to slow further.

**Layout**

Three-column horizontal flow across the middle of the slide: a rounded prompt card at left (x 2.2 to 33.6%), a central vertical stack of glowing illustrations (x 39 to 59%), and a dashcam photo at right (x 66.7 to 98%), with arrows between them. The title is centred at the top (y 7.7 to 15.8%); the main row sits at y 36 to 84%, and the caption under the right photo starts at y 70%. There is no footer, page number or logo.

**Visuals**

Left: a rounded-rectangle card with a thin grey outline containing a Chinese prompt in two lines and a small rectangular dashcam thumbnail showing a sunny residential street with trees, parked cars and a house, with the car hood across the bottom, and a dark grey circular pencil edit button overlapping its bottom-right corner. Middle-left and middle-right: two arrows, thin grey lines with small triangular heads pointing right. Centre: a tall rounded chalk-outlined card painted with brushed yellow-gold to ochre texture and two black brush-ink ideographs resembling Chinese characters; a soft gold glow surrounds it, with about six small chalk-outlined gold rectangles stacked vertically on each side. Below the card is a sketched open box with a gold-lit top band and the lettering AI FACTORY. Right: a wide-angle dashcam photograph showing a parked grey SUV at left, a pedestrian in dark clothing stepping out between parked cars, a dark grey sedan at right, beige houses, trees with a red-leaved tree, a utility pole with power lines, a blue sky, and the car hood across the bottom in grey-green; daylight, not cropped, rounded corners.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-slide image covers the slide; a white fill sits beneath it). Pure black background. White NVIDIASans Medium sans-serif title, approximately 3.7% of slide height, with regular-width letters. Body and caption text in NVIDIASans Regular, about 1.24% of slide height, white (#FFFFFF). Accent khaki-gold #A69060 for Scenario 1 and the word pedestrian. Prompt text uses HGMaruGothicMPRO at about 1.73%. Central illustration uses gold #D8C36A to #E8D880 with white chalk-sketch outlines. Left card and photos have thin light-grey (#8C8C8C) outlines with rounded corners of roughly 2% of slide width. No shadows; soft glow around the central illustration.

**Text**

Physical AI
The Foundation of Robotic Revolution
生成三個詳細且多樣化的場景，描述在接下來的
五秒內可能出現在攝影機視野中的事物。
AI FACTORY
Scenario 2
A car is stopped on a quiet suburban road, with a clear blue sky overhead and lush
green trees lining the streets. A pigeon enters the frame from the right, flying low over
the street with graceful, fluid movements. It gracefully glides past the parked cars,
then lands softly on one of the vehicles. The scene captures a serene moment, with
the pigeon's flight adding a touch of life to the tranquil neighborhood setting. A wide-
angle shot from inside the car, capturing both the bird and the surrounding
environment.

**Layout**

Three-column horizontal arrangement on a black field: a rounded prompt panel on the left, a central illustrated stack (glowing painted card above a sketched 'AI FACTORY' box) in the middle, and a photo with caption and body text on the right. Two thin arrows run between the columns at mid-height of the prompt panel. The title is centred across the top of the slide with no footer and no slide number. Left and right margins are about 2.2% of slide width; content sits in the middle band from about 36% to 84% of slide height.

**Visuals**

Illustration: a central painted card (about 12% wide by 33% tall of the slide) with a warm yellow-to-black brushed glow, outlined by a thin white sketched border, bearing two large black brush-stroke Chinese-style glyphs; a glowing halo behind it. Two stacks of small vertical rectangles, each outlined in white sketch lines and lit yellow, flank the card on the left and right. Below the card is a sketched open box with a yellow-lit lid, labelled AI FACTORY in white capitals on its front. Photograph (left panel): a small thumbnail (about 13% wide) of a suburban street with a clear blue sky, trees, and parked cars, shot from inside a vehicle; a small dark circular pencil edit icon sits on its lower-right corner. Arrows: two thin light-grey horizontal lines with small arrowheads pointing right, one from the prompt panel to the central stack and one from the central stack to the photo. Photograph (right): full-width colour photograph inside a rounded frame, taken from inside a car through the windscreen: a quiet suburban street with a clear blue sky, lush green trees, a utility pole with wires, parked silver and dark cars on the left and centre, a single-storey house with a pitched roof in the centre background, and a grey pigeon with a pink-tinged leg standing on the car bonnet in the foreground; daylight, neutral colours with green and grey dominant. A dark gradient fades the bottom of the photo edge and the lower body text into black.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-slide white #FFFFFF fill is present in the file but is covered by the full-bleed black image). Black background (#000000) with white (#FFFFFF) title and body text, a muted gold accent (#A69060) for the word pigeon and the Scenario 2 label, and dark grey outlines (#8A8A8A) for the prompt panel. Typefaces: NVIDIASans (Medium for title, Regular for body) is a geometric sans-serif; the Chinese lines use HGMaruGothicMPRO with a Yu Gothic fallback character. Title is medium weight at about 3.7% of slide height; body is regular at about 1.24% of slide height; the prompt text is about 1.73%. Prompt panel and photo frame have rounded corners of about 2% of slide width, with thin 1px-equivalent strokes; no drop shadows. Illustrated elements use a sketch style with white hand-drawn outlines and yellow glowing gradients.

**Visuals**

Left: a rounded-rectangle card outlined in light grey, containing a Chinese instruction and a rounded photo thumbnail of a top-down robotics workspace showing a round grey platform, two robot grippers at lower left and right, a spray bottle, a clear water bottle, a black lid, and a purple container on a light wood table; a small dark grey circular button with a white paperclip icon sits at the thumbnail's lower-right corner. Centre: a tall rounded panel with a brushed gold-yellow painted texture and a soft glow, bearing two large stylised black brush-ink calligraphic glyphs; on either side is a vertical stack of about six small sketch-outlined rounded rectangles with yellow glow, and below is a hand-sketched open-top tray outlined in white with a gold glow inside and the words AI FACTORY centred in white capitals. Right: a full-colour photograph, boxed with rounded corners, of a grey humanoid robot wearing a white hard hat with a small green logo, hands clasped at chest, standing behind a table with a white cup in a purple bin, a metal can, and a black shell part at right; background is a grey wire mesh wall, orange-red parts bins on shelves, and a yellow bin at upper right; lighting is even and neutral studio light; dominant colours are grey, orange-red, purple and yellow. A dark gradient fades the bottom of the paragraph into the black background. Arrows: two thin grey straight lines with small arrowheads pointing right, one from the left card to the centre panel and one from the centre panel to the photo.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 37 to 52 are dense partner architecture diagrams with tiny labels, and slides 13 and 15 are identical, as are the repeated Omniverse slides, so some pages add little.

**Text**

GB300 NVL72
Grace Blackwell NVL72
“A Thinking Machine”

**Layout**

No conventional title bar or footer. A small label sits at upper centre-left over the rack seam, and a centred two-line statement sits in the right half at about 41% to 50% of the slide height. The photograph fills the full slide and the left racks run to the bottom edge. There is no slide number, footer or logo.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed photograph of two data-centre server racks in a dark room, shown from a slight three-quarter angle. The left rack is a tall black cabinet with two gold-brass NVIDIA-branded horizontal chassis: the upper one at about 7% to 27% of slide height and x 12% to 36%, the lower one from about 42% to the bottom edge and x 12% to 36%. Each chassis has rows of vents, ports and black cables plugged in at its right side. Below and behind the upper chassis are dark stacked modules. The right rack is a recessed black cabinet at about x 42% to 56% with horizontal slats and rows of small warm-white light strips and gold-lit components visible through the openings. Lighting is low-key, warm gold and amber from inside the chassis, with black surroundings and specular highlights on the brass panels. Dominant colours: black #000000, brass gold #B9A06A, warm cream highlights #F2E2B0. The photo is not cropped inside the slide and is not boxed; it covers the full slide, with the racks running off the bottom edge. No overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Black #000000 photographic field behind the racks, with a #FFFFFF fill layer under the full-slide image. Pure black photographic field with white sans-serif text. The statement is set in a medium-weight grotesque with a large size, and the label is set in a regular weight at a much smaller size. Title and body text are both #FFFFFF. No accent colour on the text. There are no shapes, rules, rounded corners, shadows or gradients on the slide apart from the photograph's own lighting.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- photo at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#000000]: Full-slide photograph field, black background of the server image, 4000 by 2250 px (Full bleed, no crop, no overlay or tint.)
- photo at x 0, y 0, w 62.3, h 100 [#B9A06A]: Photograph of two server racks: left rack with two gold-brass NVIDIA GB300 NVL72 chassis, right rack with lit slots (Image 2741 by 2475 px, full height of the slide, rack edges run to the bottom edge; black cables overlay the chassis; no border, no rounded corners, no shadow.)

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses one large hero image with three bold comparison callouts against the previous generation to make the performance gain immediate.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent dark identity with gold titles, the single-subject hero imagery on slides 5, 14 and 16 to 19, and the 3-line site labels with power figures.

**Text**

Grace Blackwell Ultra Superchip / 40 PFLOPS TE FP4 / 1.5X GB200 / 576 GB at 16 TB/s HBM3e / 1.5X GB200 / 800 Gb/s CX8 / 2X GB200

**Layout**

Two-zone composition on a full-bleed black field. The product photograph occupies the left half, standing upright and slightly tilted, with its top near the top edge and its bottom near the bottom edge. The text block sits in the right half, vertically centred around the middle of the slide. The title is left-aligned at about 62.4% x and the spec table has two columns, a left column starting at about 64.7% x and a right column right-hanging near 85% to 92% x. There is no footer, slide number, or logo.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a Grace Blackwell Ultra Superchip compute board, shown as a dark printed circuit board tilted in three-quarter perspective, standing upright. Subject: at the top edge are two black connector blocks with gold pins; in the upper half are two large square GPU packages with gold frames and gold heatsink-like borders, each containing a multicoloured die (purple, blue, green and yellow tones). Between them are rows of black capacitors and small components. In the centre is a large square package with a grey metal frame containing a green, yellow and purple die, flanked by rectangular memory modules on the left and right. At the bottom are two smaller gold-framed chips with multicoloured dies, with connectors and heatsink mounts along the bottom edge. Lighting: soft studio lighting with specular highlights on the gold frames and metal. Dominant colours: black, dark grey, gold and yellow, with purple, blue and green on the dies. Framing: full board visible, not cropped, shown on a plain black background with no border or tint. No overlay.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (full-slide image covers a white #FFFFFF base fill). Black background with no gradient or pattern. Title is white in NVIDIA Sans Medium, sans-serif, at about 3.7% of slide height, sentence-case, no letter spacing. Spec lines are white NVIDIA Sans Regular, sans-serif, at about 1.98% of slide height, sentence-case. Spec lines are set in two columns with generous line spacing of about 4.8% of slide height between rows. No lines, shapes, rules, shadows, or corner radii. Accent colour: none apart from the gold and multicoloured hardware in the photograph.

### Slide 16

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Each partner site uses the same layout with a large power figure, location and courtesy credit, so the series reads as one comparison.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent dark identity with gold titles, the single-subject hero imagery on slides 5, 14 and 16 to 19, and the 3-line site labels with power figures.

**Text**

CoreWeave
220 MW
USA, Scaling Globally
Courtesy of CoreWeave

**Layout**

A single full-frame photograph sits inside a thin black surround with slightly rounded corners, leaving a margin of about 2.5% on the sides and about 4% at top and bottom. The three-line text block is left-aligned in the upper-left over the dark ceiling area, starting at about 7% from the left edge and 10% from the top. The caption sits bottom-right on the photo, right-aligned at about 2% from the right and 2% from the bottom edge. No title placeholder, footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One photograph of a data-centre server hall. Subject: a long row of black server racks with dense blue, yellow and white fibre and Ethernet cabling, orange connectors, and small grey-white power or control units with small screens mounted at the base of several racks. The rack in the foreground right is a closed black cabinet with a perforated front panel and a single red cable visible. On the far right is a glass-fronted enclosure. Overhead, metal cable trays and bundled cables run along the ceiling. The floor is light grey-white tiles with a grey perforated floor grille running diagonally toward the viewer. Composition: one-point perspective from a low, slightly right-of-centre viewpoint, showing the rack row receding to the left. Lighting: even cool white overhead light with a dark ceiling, and highlights on the metal fixtures. Dominant colours: black, dark grey, white, blue, yellow and orange. The photo is not cropped and is not full-bleed; it is boxed inside the black frame with no overlay or tint, though the upper-left area is darkened slightly behind the text.

**Design**

Background is solid black surrounding the photograph. The photograph is not tinted. Text is white (#FFFFFF) in NVIDIA Sans, a humanist-style geometric sans-serif: the first line is Medium weight and the other two lines are Regular. Title text is about 3.3% of slide height and the caption is about 1.7%. All text is left-aligned except the caption, which is right-aligned. Caption is Medium weight. Lines are spaced about 4% of slide height apart. No shapes, lines or shadows are added. Corners of the photo frame are slightly rounded.

### Slide 17

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent dark identity with gold titles, the single-subject hero imagery on slides 5, 14 and 16 to 19, and the 3-line site labels with power figures.

**Text**

Oracle Cloud - Supercluster / 260 MW / Indonesia, Scaling Globally / Courtesy of Oracle

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph inside a thin black border frame; three left-aligned title lines sit in the upper-left over the image, and a small credit line sits bottom-right. No visible slide number or footer.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a data-centre server hall. In the foreground, a tall black server rack with yellow and red cable management and rows of networking hardware and cabling runs down the centre-left. To the right, a second rack shows dense rows of server units with grey and green-lit components and many coloured cables. Further back, a row of darker racks recedes to the left, a red and a blue panel stand at the back, and an orange traffic cone sits on the floor. Yellow warning labels hang on the racks, one reading 'Fiber optic cable' area text. The floor is light grey, glossy and reflective; ceiling trays and overhead lights are visible. Composition is a wide landscape shot with slight depth-of-field softening at the edges; lighting is cool overhead fluorescent with reflections on the floor. Dominant colours: black, dark grey, light grey, with red and yellow accents. The photo is cropped to a rounded-corner rectangle with a thin dark border, not full-bleed to the slide edge.

**Design**

Background is white (#FFFFFF) surrounding a near-black frame (#000000 margin). Title text is white (#FFFFFF) in NVIDIASans-Medium, body lines are white in NVIDIASans-Regular, all set over the photograph. Title is about 3.3% of slide height for the first line and the two following lines are the same size, so there is no size hierarchy beyond weight. Sans-serif typeface, regular and medium weights. Photo has a small corner radius and a thin light border. No shadows or gradients on the slide itself; the photo has a subtle dark vignette at the top-left to support the white text.

xAI - Colossus / 150 MW / Memphis, Tennessee / Courtesy of xAI

**Visuals**

Aerial photograph (drone-style, high oblique angle from the south-east looking north-west) of a large single-storey, long, cream-coloured data centre building with a flat light-grey metal roof studded with rows of small roof vents and skylights. In front of the building's long side, rows of grey industrial cooling units and white/grey rectangular containers sit on concrete pads, with pale blue-white tanker trucks parked on the right-hand lot and a white cylindrical tank at upper right. Left side shows a fenced electrical substation with transformers and a lattice pylon, a parking lot with cars at top, and a green flat field occupying the lower-left and bottom. Lighting is low-angle warm daylight casting long shadows toward the upper-left. Dominant colours: green field, light grey roof, cream walls, dark asphalt. The photograph is full-bleed within the frame, not cropped at the subject, with a subtle dark vignette at the corners and no tint or overlay beyond the white text.

Background: Solid black (#000000) surround with the aerial photograph filling the inner frame. Black surround (#000000) framing a photograph; the photograph has a thin rounded border of about 1 pixel in dark grey. Title and body text are white (#FFFFFF) in a geometric humanist sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans style, similar to Helvetica). Title line is Medium weight, the next two lines Regular, all at about 3.3% of slide height for the title block; credit line is Medium at about 1.7% of slide height. No accent colours, no shapes, no shadows, no gradients.

**Layout**

A single full-bleed-within-frame aerial photograph fills almost the entire slide, inset from the edges by a thin black margin. There is no separate title area, footer, or slide number.

**Visuals**

Aerial photograph of a large data-centre construction site in Abilene, Texas, shot from an oblique high angle looking down across the site. Subject: seven large, low, flat-roofed buildings with pale tan or beige roofs arranged in a staggered grid, linked by a smaller cross-shaped tan roofed block at centre. Along the long sides of several buildings run rows of grey cooling or mechanical units. Around the buildings are red-brown dirt roads, construction equipment, cranes, scaffolding, stacked materials, portable white tents and trailers, a large parking area full of parked vehicles at upper right, two white storage tanks at lower right, and a fenced substation-like area with grey pipes and equipment at lower left. Composition: wide landscape frame, the site fills the centre, with the camera tilted so the roofs recede toward the top. Lighting: bright, even midday sunlight with soft shadows. Dominant colours: rust-red earth, pale beige roofs, grey machinery, and some green scrub at the edges. The photograph is not cropped at the subject, is boxed with a thin light edge and slightly rounded corners, and has no overlay or tint; the title text is placed directly on the earth at top-left.

Background: Solid black #000000 surround framing the photograph (a white #FFFFFF fill sits behind the image). Black surround frame with a near-black background. Photograph occupies a rounded-corner box with a thin light grey edge. Title and subtitle text are white in a clean grotesque sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans style), title in Medium weight and the two subtitle lines in Regular weight, all set at about 3.3% of slide height. The credit line is white Medium sans-serif at about 1.7% of slide height. No shapes, lines, gradients or shadows are visible apart from the photograph's edge.

### Slide 20

**Text**

_(no text on this slide)_

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph with no title, text, footer or slide number. The photo sits in a box inset from the slide edges, leaving a thin black margin on all sides (about 2.3% left and right, 4.5% top, about 4.5% bottom), with the subject filling the centre of the frame.

**Visuals**

One photograph, not cropped and not full-bleed, boxed inside a black surround with a thin light edge. Subject: two workers in anti-static smocks and grey gloves handle a beige-grey 1U server chassis with a perforated vent panel, held horizontally between them in front of an open server rack. The left worker is a young person with ear hoops, black-rimmed glasses, a green wristband, looking down at the chassis with both gloved hands on it. The right worker is an older man with glasses and a badge clipped to his smock, looking at the chassis, with one gloved hand raised in front of him. Behind them a tall black rack holds rows of blue-grey drive trays and cable-managed bays receding into depth, with a lower section of stacked grey crates. Composition: eye-level, medium shot, subjects at left and right edges, chassis at centre, symmetrical framing. Lighting: soft, cool overhead fluorescent light with some bright highlights on the rack and in the background. Dominant colours: white and light grey smocks, blue-grey and steel rack, beige chassis, black rack frames, touches of green on the wristband and gloves. Overlay or tint: none; no text overlaid.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 surround around the photograph, with white #FFFFFF fill beneath the full-slide layers. Black surround frame with no text. No typefaces, no titles, no body text, no accents besides the photograph's own colours. Photo has a thin light-grey outline, square corners, no shadow, no gradient. Full-slide layers beneath the photo include a white fill (#FFFFFF) and two full-slide images, which are not visibly showing beyond the black surround.

Lighting is low-key, with the warm glow from the rack modules as the main light source and the upper area fading into black. ### Slide 22

**Text**

Grace Blackwell NVL72
“One Giant GPU”

**Layout**

Centered composition on a black field. Two lines of title stacked at the top centre, roughly 2% to 10% of slide height, with a single circular photograph centred below it, filling about 53% of slide width. No footer, page number, or logo is visible; margins are roughly 23% on each side for the image.

**Visuals**

One photograph, a top-down rendering of a large multi-die GPU package, cropped to a circle and set on black. Subject: a rectangular package with a dark outer frame. Along the top and bottom edges run silver-grey bump or heat-spreader strips with fine grid texture. Between them is a grid of gold-coloured compute blocks, arranged in columns with a central gold vertical block cluster on each side of the middle. A horizontal band of small gold square connector pads runs across the centre, with a bundle of gold fibre-like interconnect wires crossing the middle. Small gold pads line the left and right edges. Composition: centred, frontal, filling most of the circle. Lighting: directional studio light giving highlights on the metal strips and warm gold tones on the blocks. Dominant colours: gold #C8A24A, silver-grey #B4B8BE, black #000000. Circle background: grey-white silicon-like sheen with diagonal bright and dark streaks, roughly #9A9CA3 to #F0F0F2. Not cropped within the package; the circle masks the outer edges. Boxed as a circle on black, no overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background is solid black #000000 with a full-bleed photograph. Title and subtitle are white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Medium, a clean geometric grotesque sans-serif with medium weight. Title text height is about 3.7% of slide height, the same size as the second line. No accent colours in the text. Line spacing is tight, about 4.5% between baselines. No shapes, lines, corner radii, shadows or gradients are present apart from the circular photo mask.

### Slide 23

**Text**

Announcing NVLink Fusion
NVIDIA AI Factory Plus Custom ASIC
Custom ASIC with
NVLink Fusion IP

**Layout**

Single full-bleed black composition with no footer or slide number. A two-line centered title sits across the top band (about 2% to 10% of height). A large circular photograph occupies the lower-centre, and a magnified detail callout sits at the upper right with a small caption beneath it. Dashed leader lines connect the highlighted die block to the callout.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a large custom silicon die (ASIC) shown as a rectangular chip on a circular, grey-to-white blurred wafer-like backdrop. The die has a silver-grey row of small square cells along its top and bottom edges. Its central field holds rows of purple (#8A2BE2-like, magenta-violet) compute blocks alternating with rows of tan/gold (#C1B485-like) blocks, with a gold bundled-wire band running horizontally across the middle. A thin gold strip of small blocks runs down both left and right edges. One purple block near the upper-right of the die is outlined with a thin white rounded rectangle. Two dashed white leader lines run from this outlined block to a rounded white-bordered inset. The inset is a magnified, portrait-oriented view of the same block showing purple core arrays in four columns, a grey-white header band at the top and a gold IP/logic band at the bottom. The caption below the inset reads Custom ASIC with / NVLink Fusion IP in gold. Lighting is flat and even; the composition is centred on the die with the inset at the right. The die is not cropped; the circular backdrop is cut by the slide edges only at its lower and left parts.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-slide background, with a full-bleed 4000 by 2250 px image covering the slide. Pure black background with no gradient. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) NVIDIASans Medium, a clean geometric sans-serif, regular weight, about 3.7% of slide height for both title lines, centred. Caption text is gold-tan #C1B485, NVIDIASans Medium, about 1.5% of slide height, centred. Die photo has a grey-white (#D9D9D9 to #FFFFFF highlights, #1A1A1A shadows) wafer backdrop. Callout inset has a white (#FFFFFF) rounded border about 1 to 1.5 pixels thick with a corner radius of roughly 15% of its width. Leader lines are thin dashed light grey-white (#E0E0E0). No shadows.

**Layout**

Centred two-line title sits at the top of the slide, spanning roughly 32% to 68% of the width with about 2% top margin. Below it, a large circular photo of the chip sits left of centre, occupying roughly 29% to 71% of the width and 19% to 95% of the height, with a zoom callout box at the upper right (about 83% to 92% across, 14% to 38% down). The callout caption is centred beneath the callout box. No footer, page number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

One photograph of a custom CPU die, with an enlargement callout. Main photo: a top-down view of a large rectangular chip die, roughly 30% of the slide width, centred on the grey circular backdrop. The die has a black outer border; grey silicon I/O strips run along the top and bottom edges; the core area holds rows of gold-tan rectangular blocks, two vertical columns of blue-teal tiles (one upper-centre, one lower-centre), a horizontal band of gold interconnect wiring across the middle, and small gold blocks along the left and right edges. A small square at about 51% to 53% across and 41% to 45% down is outlined with a thin white rectangle, marking the area enlarged in the callout. Thin white dashed leader lines run from this square to the top-left and bottom-left corners of the callout box. Callout: a white-outlined rounded rectangle containing a magnified view of four blue-teal compute tiles arranged in a 2x2 grid, with a horizontal gold strip of interconnect logic running between the upper and lower pairs. Lighting: flat, even, top-down illumination of the die. Dominant colours: black, silver-grey, gold-tan #C1B485, teal-blue. Framing: full chip visible, not cropped. Backdrop: a circular crop of an abstract grey and white metallic swirl with soft black shadows, set on the black slide. Caption under the callout in gold #C1B485 text, two centred lines.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-slide image (the white fill beneath it is covered). Black full-bleed background with a white underlying fill. Title text is white #FFFFFF in NVIDIA Sans Medium, a clean sans-serif, regular-to-medium weight, sized about 3.7% of slide height for both title lines. Caption text is gold #C1B485 in NVIDIA Sans Medium at about 1.5% of slide height. Callout outline is white, about 0.3% of slide height stroke, with rounded corners of about 1.5% of slide width. Leader lines are thin white dashed strokes. No shadows, no gradients on text; the backdrop circle is a photographic grey gradient.

There is no footer, page number or logo.

**Visuals**

Photograph of NVIDIA-style hardware, a three-quarter studio render on a black background, shown as a single image spanning the slide. From left to right: DGX Spark, a small flat light-tan box at the bottom left; DGX Station, an open-faced light-tan cube with a recessed panel; a tall black rack labelled GB200 / GB300 NVL72 with glowing gold-lit modules and cabling in its front; a second black rack with a dotted gold LED strip and gold-lit vertical slots; RTX PRO Laptop, an open silver-white laptop on the floor; RTX PRO Workstation, a dark grey-black tower with gold-brushed drive trays; RTX PRO Server, a tall light-grey and beige cabinet with horizontal slats; DGX B200/B300, a dark rack with two gold-glitter blocks; AI Storage Server, a narrow beige cabinet with horizontal slats. Lighting is soft studio spotlighting with warm gold highlights on the Arm racks and the DGX B200/B300. Dominant colours are black, warm beige #C8B99A, light grey #D9D9D9 and gold #C1B485. Not cropped; full group visible within the slide, and it is not boxed or tinted.

**Design**

Background pure black #000000. Title in NVIDIASans Medium, a sans-serif geometric grotesque, white #FFFFFF, about 4.07% of slide height, single line, sentence case. Section labels Arm Platforms and x86 Platforms in NVIDIASans Medium, gold #C1B485, about 2.22% of slide height. Captions in NVIDIASans Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 1.48% of slide height, centred, two lines each. Behind them are a dark kitchen counter with a warm amber LED strip, a white vase, a wooden shelf with small objects, and a large window on the right with sunlight streaming through. Lighting is warm, low-angle golden sunlight with amber highlights and deep shadows. Dominant colours are amber, ochre, warm brown and dark charcoal. Composition is wide landscape with the laptop centred and the window framing the right edge. The photo is not cropped and is boxed by the thin black border, with no overlay or tint.

Eight rounded-circle cartoon portraits of one man with grey hair and glasses, in a single row inside a dark panel at the top of the main column, left to right: (1) in a white shirt holding a yellow folder, (2) in a black t-shirt reading Hello World with black headphones, (3) in a navy suit with arms crossed, (4) in a white lab coat and safety goggles holding a green flask, (5) in a yellow hard hat and orange-yellow safety vest, (6) in a red traditional-style jacket, (7) in blue scrubs with a stethoscope holding a clipboard and a pill bottle, (8) in a green plaid shirt and navy beanie. Three-row diagram of gold bars: NEMO and OMNIVERSE on the top row, CUDA-X, AI-Q, DOCA in the middle row, COMPUTE, STORAGE, NETWORKING in the bottom row.

### Slide 30

**Text**

_(no text on this slide)_

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a single photograph inset from the edges, centred horizontally, occupying about 95% of the width and 91% of the height, with a thin gold outline following the photo's rounded corners. There is no title, text, footer or slide number, and the image sits in the middle of the slide with narrow black margins on all sides.

**Visuals**

One photograph, a studio render of an open server rack seen from the front and slightly above. Eight gold-bronze graphics accelerator cards are pulled partly out of their slots and fanned outward in two symmetric groups: four on the left leaning outward to the left and four on the right leaning outward to the right, each card's long edge running roughly toward the viewer. In the centre the cards sit upright in a row, with their gold-edged connector ends at the bottom of each card. Behind them the dark rack interior shows a row of four grey cable connector ports along the top edge and dark horizontal panels. Below the cards a dark grey server chassis tray spans the lower centre, with a long flat metal cover plate and beneath it a row of five dark grey drive bays along the bottom edge. Lighting is a single spotlight from above, with warm highlights on the gold card surfaces and deep shadows elsewhere. Dominant colours are black, bronze gold and dark charcoal grey. The photo is not cropped and is boxed inside the thin gold frame, with no overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000, with a white fill layer beneath it fully covered by black image layers. Solid black background with a thin gold rounded-rectangle outline around the photograph, about 0.1% of slide height in stroke width, colour gold #C9A227. No title or body typography. Corner radius on the outline is small, about 1% of slide height. No shadows or gradients on the slide itself; the photograph carries its own lighting.

One product photograph of a NVIDIA RTX PRO Server, a dark grey rack chassis, shown at an angle from the front-left. Behind them sits a black circuit board with four GPU packages and eight card-edge connectors along its front edge. The lower front of the chassis has three rows of mesh-front hot-swap bays and fan modules, with a row of dark drive trays in the middle row. Lighting is studio-style, with soft highlights on the metal and gold heatsinks against a pure black background. The subject is complete and not cropped, floating with no border. A pure black backdrop covers the whole slide. Dominant colours are black, dark grey, gunmetal, and gold-tan (#C1B485). Headline stats are NVIDIA Sans Medium at about 2.2% of slide height. Supporting lines are NVIDIA Sans Regular at about 2.0% of slide height. Text is centred on a shared vertical axis. The title sits at top centre of the right column (y 16.8%); there is no footer, slide number or source line. Left margin about 5.8% and right margin about 8% of slide width.

**Visuals**

Left: a photograph of a black floor-standing server rack seen straight on, front view, full height, boxed (not cropped, no overlay). Top section is a compute stack of about 9 dark grey 1U/2U nodes with perforated grilles; the middle section is a single networking switch with two grey handles and a dense port panel; the bottom section is about 8 storage bays with dark drive faces. Soft studio lighting from the front, dominant colours near-black and charcoal grey. Gold bracket lines (#C1B485, about 1 px stroke) on the left of the rack mark Compute (top, about y 24 to 49%), Networking (about y 50.6 to 56.9%) and Storage (about y 58.3 to 83.3%). Right: a diagram. A circular white-outlined portrait of a man with grey hair and glasses in a dark top (illustrated avatar, about 51.3 to 56.2% x, 34.9 to 43.6% y) connects by two horizontal arrows labelled Prompt (top arrow, pointing right to the loop) and Response (bottom arrow, pointing left back to the portrait). The arrows run from the portrait's right edge to a thin white circular arc. The arc is open at the upper left and carries Critique (upper left, arrow head near the loop) and Plan (upper right, arrow head) as labels; the NVIDIA green cube icon sits at the centre of the arc above the label NVIDIA Llama / Nemotron Reason. The arc's bottom carries the label Tool Use with an arrowhead pointing right-up at its right end. A vertical white line drops from the Tool Use label down to a horizontal white bracket (thin stroke, small downward ticks) that splits into five drops, one to each of five icons: an orange calculator icon (Calculator), an orange database cylinder (Web Search), a green NVIDIA cube (Semantic Query), an orange database cylinder (SQL Query) and a green NVIDIA cube (Generate Summary). Icon fills: orange #EF4E1E, green #76B900 with darker green shading. Logos below: row 1 (y about 78 to 80%) reads ddn (white circular ring mark plus wordmark), DELL Technologies (white wordmark with a blue-free all-white logotype), Hewlett Packard Enterprise (white wordmark with a small rectangular mark above), Hitachi Vantara (white roundel mark plus wordmark), H3C (white wordmark), IBM (white block letters). Row 2 (y about 85 to 88%) reads NetApp (white mark plus wordmark), NUTANIX (white wordmark), PURE STORAGE (white mark plus wordmark with registered symbol), VAST (white inverted-triangle mark plus wordmark), WEKA (white slanted-stroke mark plus wordmark). All logos rendered white, in one line per row, evenly spaced.

Background: Solid black #000000 (opaque full-slide image of the same black beneath a #FFFFFF fill, so the visible colour is #000000). Pure black field (#000000) throughout with no gradients, shadows or textures. ### Slide 36

**Review notes**

- Review: Many slides lack a statement of what the viewer should take away, and the slide 36 table is illegible.

**Text**

NVIDIA Top Leaderboard Open Models and Datasets / 15X Faster / 50% Greater / #1 / Faster Data Extraction / NeMo Retriever / Open Reasoning Models / Llama Nemotron / Higher Retrieval Accuracy / NeMo Retriever / Hugging Face / Search models, datasets, users... / Models / Datasets / Spaces / Posts / Docs / Enterprise / Pricing / Main / Tasks / Libraries / Languages / Licenses / Other / Modalities / 3D / Audio / Geospatial / Image / Tabular / Text / Time-series / Video / Size (rows) / <1K / >1T / Format / json / csv / parquet / imagefolder / soundfolder / webdataset / text / arrow / Datasets 390,716 / Filter by name / Full-text search / Sort: Trending / DMindAI/DMind_Benchmark / openbmb/Ultra-FineWeb / nvidia/Nemotron-CrossThink / sunhaonlp/ZeroSearch_dataset / tokyotech-llm/swallow-code / fka/awesome-chatgpt-prompts / Qwen/PolyMath / HuggingFaceFW/fineweb / nvidia/OpenCodeReasoning / nvidia/OpenMathReasoning / PrimeIntellect/INTELLECT-2-RL-Dataset / FreedomIntelligence/medical-o1-reasoning-SFT / nvidia/Llama-Nemotron-Post-Training-Dataset / HuggingFaceM4/the_cauldron / Salesforce/APIGen-MT-5k / tokyotech-llm/swallow-math

**Layout**

A centred headline runs across the top at about 3 percent from the slide top, above a three-column row of stat blocks (left, centre with laurel badge, right) that sits in the upper half. Below the stats, a wide framed Hugging Face dataset-browser screenshot spans roughly 16 to 90 percent of slide width and runs off the bottom edge of the slide. No footer, page number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-slide black background. Centre-top: a gold laurel wreath enclosing the text #1, with the laurel stems meeting at the bottom. Below the stats row, a dark screenshot of the Hugging Face Datasets page, shown in a rounded-corner window frame with a thin grey border, cropped at the bottom edge. The screenshot shows a left filter panel (Main tab selected, Modalities chips, a Size (rows) slider, Format chips), a central list of dataset cards with name, viewer label, update time, download and like counts, and a right column of dimmed cards. Five cards (nvidia/Nemotron-CrossThink, nvidia/OpenCodeReasoning, nvidia/OpenMathReasoning, nvidia/Llama-Nemotron-Post-Training-Dataset) have bright beige-gold outlines and glow; the other cards are dimmed grey. Soft gold-tinted glow images sit behind the headline stats. Overall tone is black with beige-gold #C1B485 accents.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with no gradient or pattern.

### Slide 37

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 37 to 52 are dense partner architecture diagrams with tiny labels, and slides 13 and 15 are identical, as are the repeated Omniverse slides, so some pages add little.

**Text**

VAST / DataEngine / User or Machine / Prompt / Plan / Reflect / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever / Extraction / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever / Embedding / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever / Reranking / NVIDIA NIM / NVIDIA Llama Nemotron / Reason / Refine / Report / Actions / Images / Documents / Videos / Chat / Email / Vectors / Data Catalog / EDW / Internet / DataStore / DataBase

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with no title, footer or slide number. A thin grey rounded-corner frame spans almost the whole slide, with the VAST logo box centred on its top edge. Inside, a wide dark DataEngine panel sits across the upper-middle, with DataStore and DataBase panels in a row along the bottom and a server-rack photo bleeding off the lower-left corner.

**Visuals**

Diagram on black. DataEngine panel (#111111, rounded corners): four green NVIDIA NeMo-style cube icons in a row, left to right NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Extraction, NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding, NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking, NVIDIA NIM, joined by short grey arrows pointing right. A fifth green cube labelled NVIDIA Llama Nemotron Reason sits to the right, linked to dark rounded boxes Plan, Reflect and Refine: Plan to Reflect with arrow right, Reflect down to Refine with arrow left, Refine up to Plan with arrow up. An outline person icon labelled User or Machine Prompt sits above, with a grey arrow down into Plan. An arrow from Reason at right points to Report / Actions, shown by outline chart and gear icons. A grey elbow line runs from Refine down and left to Vectors area and right to Internet. DataStore panel (#111111, rounded): outline icons Images (person in frame), Documents (line chart), Videos (play screen), Chat (speech bubbles), Email (envelope), with arrows from Documents up to NeMo Retriever Extraction and from Embedding down and right to Vectors. DataBase panel (#111111): outline icons Vectors (cube), Data Catalog (grid table), EDW (cylinder), with arrow from Internet pointing left into EDW and grey arrows from the elbow line down into Data Catalog and up to NeMo Reranking. Internet panel (#111111): outline globe icon labelled Internet. Photograph at lower left: black server rack in perspective with rows of lit drives and a cyan-blue light strip, dark grey metal, cropped at the slide's bottom edge and boxed with a dark grey bezel. Logos: VAST chevron mark with wordmark in white inside rounded box; small VAST chevron marks in cyan beside DataEngine, DataStore and DataBase labels; NVIDIA green cube icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide (a white fill lies beneath the full-slide image).

Will construct the action plan using observed deal patterns and testimonials relevant for the region/industry, and will highlight that GTM strategy was not directly accessible.

**Layout**

A dark software-UI screenshot framed inside a black surround, with the app window occupying about 96 percent of the width and 91 percent of the height. Two columns: a narrow left chat panel (about x 7 to 33 percent) with a darker header and input box, and a wide right area (about x 35 to 85 percent) holding the agent output starting near the top. The VAST logo sits top-left at about x 8 y 7 percent. There is no slide title, footer, page number or footnote.

**Visuals**

A screenshot of a chat-agent interface, not a photograph. Left panel: a chat header with a speech-bubble icon and the word Chat; a user message bubble with a circular avatar showing the letter U; a 'Review Thinking Process' row with a small robot icon and a chevron; a grouped card listing four collapsible rows, each with a downward chevron: Outline, CRM Retrieval, Multimodal Data Retrieval, Build Action Plan; a rounded input box with placeholder Reply to your agent..., a 'Start New Chat' link with a small circle icon, and a blue circular send button with a paper-plane glyph at its right. Right area: a small VAST triangle-style icon beside the heading Create Action Plan, a small pill labelled Step 4 with a checkmark, then two text blocks of agent output. No charts, tables or photographs. The VAST logo is a white inverted-triangle mark with the wordmark VAST in spaced capitals. The full-slide black surround and a thin gold hairline frame the window.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 surround with the navy #0B1A36 application window behind the content. Dark-mode UI. Background is deep navy #0B1A36 for the window, with the left chat panel slightly lighter at #13233F and the input box and task card at #1B2D4D. Primary text is light grey-white #E5E7EB; secondary and output body text is grey #A9B4C6; the user bubble text is #E5E7EB. Accent is blue #3B82F6 on the send button and a muted blue-grey on the step pill. Typeface is a neo-grotesque sans-serif similar to Helvetica or Inter, regular weight for body and medium weight for headings and row labels. Body text is about 0.9 percent of slide height, headings about 1.1 percent, the VAST wordmark about 1.3 percent with wide letter-spacing. Shapes have rounded corners of about 1 to 1.5 percent of slide height, no visible shadows, flat fills, and a thin low-contrast border around the input box. The outer window has a thin gold #C9A24A hairline border with rounded corners.

**Text**

Client Applications / Agent / Prompt / Response / Identity & Access Management / NVIDIA Llama Nemotron Reasoning / DELL AI Data Platform With NVIDIA / Data Platform Services / Storage Access Control & Identity Management / Federated SQL Engine / AI Data Pipelines / Data Processing Engine / Unstructured Data Engine / Web Search / External Data Sources Structured & Unstructured / RAG Pipeline / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking / Index NVIDIA cuVS / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Extraction / Dataset Interface & Access Control / Storage Platform / Project Lightning | PowerScale | ObjectScale | PowerProtect / Vector Data / Iceberg-based S3 Tables / Semi-structured & Unstructured Data / Tensor Data / PowerScale Metadata IQ / Data Resiliency, Protection, Deduplication

**Layout**

A full-slide black frame with a thin grey rounded outline inset about 2% from the edges, with the Dell Technologies logo box centred at the top edge. Below it, a left column holds client application icons and labels, a centre-right dark panel titled DELL AI Data Platform With NVIDIA holds stacked service and pipeline bands, and a right column holds Web Search and External Data Sources. A server rack image sits bottom-left, overlapping the frame edge. There is no visible footer, page number or slide number.

**Visuals**

Diagram on black background with white and light-grey line icons and green NVIDIA cubes. Shapes and connections: Agent icon (person outline) with a down arrow to Prompt icon (speech bubble) and a down arrow to Identity & Access Management (padlock); a horizontal arrow from Identity & Access Management to the NVIDIA Llama Nemotron Reasoning green cube; an arrow from that cube up to Response (speech bubble) and a line from Response up and left back to Agent. Between the Llama cube and the RAG Pipeline, two horizontal arrows run to a vertical dashed bar labelled Dataset Interface & Access Control. Data Platform Services band contains five gear or lock icons with labels: Storage Access Control & Identity Management (padlock), Federated SQL Engine, AI Data Pipelines, Data Processing Engine, Unstructured Data Engine. A bidirectional arrow links Web Search (globe icon) to the services band at the right, and a left-pointing arrow links External Data Sources (document icon) to the right side of the RAG panel. A vertical bidirectional arrow runs from the Unstructured Data Engine down to PowerScale Metadata IQ (server icon). The RAG Pipeline is a green-outlined rounded rectangle containing four items: NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking (green cube), Index NVIDIA cuVS (orange-outlined square icon with orange nodes and an orange bar beneath), NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding (green cube), NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Extraction (green cube). A vertical bidirectional arrow links the RAG Pipeline to the Storage Platform panel below it. The Storage Platform panel contains four cylinder icons labelled Vector Data, Iceberg-based S3 Tables, Semi-structured & Unstructured Data, Tensor Data, all joined by a bracket line leading to the caption Data Resiliency, Protection, Deduplication. Bottom-left photograph: a tall black data-centre storage rack with lit blue-white drive bays, cropped at the slide bottom edge and overlapping the outer frame.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the entire slide, with the frame and panels layered on top. Black background #000000. Titles and headings in white #FFFFFF, bold sans-serif (Dell-style grotesque, close to Helvetica Bold). Body labels in light grey #D0D0D0 regular sans-serif, approximately 1.2% of slide height. Panel titles about 1.6% of slide height. Accent colours: NVIDIA green #76B900, orange #F26B1D for the cuVS icon, Dell logo white. Panels are dark grey #1A1A1A rounded rectangles with corner radius about 1% of width, no shadows. The RAG Pipeline outline is green #76B900 1px rounded rectangle. Connector lines are thin grey #8A8A8A about 1px with small arrowheads. Outer frame is a thin grey #6E6E6E rounded rectangle with a faint warm gradient on the top and bottom edges.

**Layout**

Single full-bleed dark diagram with no title placeholder. The Hitachi Vantara logo box is centred at the top edge, about 1.2% from the top. A thin rounded outline frame encloses the diagram from roughly 2% to 98% horizontally and 7.5% to 88% vertically. Inside it, a top-left group (iQ Studio Agent) and a larger top-right group (iQ Studio Agent Runtime) sit above a lower-centre Data Load group, with a server rack photo at the bottom-left corner. No page number, footer or source line is visible.

**Visuals**

Diagram of a left-to-right and top-to-bottom data flow. Rounded dark grey panels: iQ Studio Agent (contains iQ Studio Hitachi iQ icon, Access Control icon, iQ Studio Time Agent icon, arrows left to right), iQ Studio Agent Runtime (Question icon, NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding cube, NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking cube, Question & Context icon, NVIDIA NIM cube, Chat History circle, Answer icon), and Data Load (VSP One database cylinder, NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Extraction cube, NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding cube, VectorDB cylinder, calendar-with-gear icon). Arrows: User to iQ Studio Hitachi iQ to Access Control to iQ Studio Time Agent to Question to NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding (labelled Question With Time Range) to Question & Context (via horizontal arrow) to NVIDIA NIM. Answer returns from NVIDIA NIM along the top to Answer icon and then left and down to iQ Studio Time Agent. Time Agent sends a line down and left labelled Response With Cited Source Link back up to User. Retriever Embedding sends a line down labelled Embedding With Time Range Metadata to VectorDB. Reranking connects up to the Retriever Embedding horizontal line and Chat History arrow points up into Question & Context. Calendar icon sends a line up labelled Filtered Response With Temporal Version to Reranking. Data Load arrows: VSP One Object & File to NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Extraction labelled Versioned Enterprise Data; Extraction to NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding; to VectorDB labelled Embedding With Time Metadata; VectorDB to calendar icon labelled Embeddings With Matching Metadata. Icons: user photograph of a smiling man with short dark hair and glasses in dark clothing, in a circle with thin red ring; line-art brain icons in white; red outline padlock shield; blue speech-bubble icons; green NVIDIA cubes with node networks; gold outline circle with dots for Chat History; red and white database cylinders; purple calendar with orange gear. Server rack photograph at bottom-left: black storage cabinet with grey checkered front panels and small indicator lights, cropped at slide bottom edge.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-slide black image covers a white #FFFFFF fill beneath it). Black background. Titles are bold white sans-serif (Helvetica/Arial bold) at about 1.7% of slide height. Body labels are regular white sans-serif (Helvetica/Arial) at about 1.3% of slide height, with sub-labels around 1.0%. Accent colours: NVIDIA green #76B900, blue line icons #3FA9F5, red #D7191F, orange gear #F26A21, gold frame and gold Chat History circle #C9A227. Panels are dark grey #111111 with rounded corners of about 1.5% of slide width. Connector lines are thin light grey #9A9A9A, about 0.1% of slide height, with small arrowheads. Hitachi Vantara logo box has a rounded rectangle with a thin light grey #D0D0D0 outline and black fill. No shadows or gradients on panels.

### Slide 41

**Text**

IBM / PDF / S3/NFS / IBM Storage / Scale + AFM / Kafka / Triggers / CAS / Runtime / Data / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever / Extract / Embed / Vectorize / Index / Vector / Database / Vectorized / Re-rank / [1 0, 3, 4] / [1, 3, 8, 6] / [9, 4, 8, 7] / [5, 3, 8, 0] / 1. / 2. / 3. / n. / CAS Search / API / api / Query / Plan / Refine / Reflect / NVIDIA Llama / Nemotron / Reason / Generate / NVIDIA NIM / Answer / PDF / Data / Metadata / Access / Control / Top-K / Enterprise Metadata and / Role-Based Access Control / NVIDIA GPU Operator / Red Hat / OpenShift / NVIDIA Network Operator

**Layout**

Full-width architecture diagram in a single wide frame: left-to-right data pipeline (PDF sources, storage, Kafka, CAS Runtime) feeding a dark NeMo Retriever panel in the centre, then CAS Search API and a Query-to-Answer loop on the right, with a shared metadata/access-control node and an operator bar along the bottom. The IBM logo sits in a rounded box centred on the top edge of the outer frame, with no title text and no slide number. The rack photograph sits in the bottom-left corner, overlapping the frame edge.

**Visuals**

Diagram with blue line icons and green NVIDIA hexagon logos. Shapes and labels: a PDF document icon labelled PDF (top) with a downward arrow to an S3/NFS database cylinder; an arrow to an IBM Storage Scale + AFM storage icon; an arrow to a Kafka icon labelled Triggers on the arrow to a CAS Runtime gear-in-window icon; an arrow labelled Data to a dark rounded panel titled NVIDIA NeMo Retriever (with NVIDIA logo at top). Inside the panel, a left-to-right chain of document icons with arrows: Extract, Embed, Vectorize, Index, Vector Database (cylinder), Vectorized Re-rank (blue numbered list 1. 2. 3. n. with an arrow back to Vector Database); arrow from Vector Database upward to CAS Runtime is not present, instead Top-K arrow goes from Vector Database down to a shield/person icon labelled Enterprise Metadata and Role-Based Access Control. A second PDF icon (lower left) has a line labelled Data running right to the CAS Runtime arrow. A line labelled Metadata runs from the storage icon down and right to the shield icon. A bidirectional arrow runs between the NeMo panel and the CAS Search API box (blue api icon), which connects by a line labelled Access Control down to the shield icon. Right side: Query speech-bubble icon with a downward arrow to a circular loop labelled Plan at top, Refine at left, Reflect at right, containing the NVIDIA Llama Nemotron Reason logo; a downward arrow labelled Generate to the NVIDIA NIM logo; a downward arrow to an Answer speech-bubble icon. Bottom bar: rounded dark panel holding NVIDIA GPU Operator logo (green hexagon gear), Red Hat OpenShift logo (red hat and wordmark), NVIDIA Network Operator logo (green hexagon gear). Photograph: a tall black server rack with blue-lit rows of hardware, shown front-on, full height of its box, sharp focus, lit from its own indicators, cropped at the bottom edge of the slide and overlapping the outer frame line at lower left; no tint.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the slide (a full-slide image sits over a white #FFFFFF fill). Dark theme: black background with white and light grey line work, blue (#4589FF) icons and labels, NVIDIA green (#76B900) logos, and red (#EE0000) Red Hat mark. Thin light-grey (#8D8D8D) rounded outer frame line, 0.5-pixel strokes, grey connector arrows (#8D8D8D). Dark grey (#161616) rounded-corner panels for the NeMo Retriever area and the operator bar, with about 8 percent corner radius. Labels in IBM Plex Sans regular, white (#FFFFFF) for node labels and blue (#4589FF) for pipeline labels, about 1.2 percent of slide height; the IBM logo is a white striped wordmark in a rounded outlined box with a light grey (#C6C6C6) border. No shadows or gradients visible.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100: Full-slide background image, black (Covers the whole slide over a white #FFFFFF base fill; renders as solid black #000000.)
