### Slide 42

**Text**

NetApp / Data Compute Nodes / Sync & Detect Updates / Catalog Metadata / Data Governance / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking / Storage Compute Nodes / High Speed Networking / Disaggregated Storage Enclosures / Extract Updates / Classify and Anonymize / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding / Vector DB / Access Control / Auto Logging / Traceability and Versioning / User / NetApp AI Agent / AI Agent / AI Agent / NVIDIA Llama Nemotron Reason

**Layout**

A full-bleed dark architecture diagram with no title text. A thin rounded frame spans nearly the whole slide, with the NetApp logo box centred on its top edge. The central Data Compute Nodes panel sits in the middle; storage components run down the left, a server rack image sits bottom-left, and user and agent components run down the right. Elements are placed on a free-form diagram grid with no footer, page number or slide number.

**Visuals**

Architecture diagram. Left column: a Storage Compute Nodes icon (cyan gear with NetApp mark) above a 'High Speed Networking' label, with a bidirectional arrow to the central panel and vertical bidirectional arrows above and below to a Disaggregated Storage Enclosures icon (cyan stacked-disk cylinder with NetApp mark). Bottom-left: a photograph of a NetApp storage rack, a tall dark server cabinet with a front grille, rows of blue-lit drive bays and white NetApp labels, shown full height, cropped at the slide bottom edge, unboxed. Central panel: rounded dark grey box holding the 'Data Compute Nodes' title beside a cyan gear icon. Top row, left to right: Sync & Detect Updates (document icon) with a right arrow to Catalog Metadata (document with gear); Data Governance (document with check); NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking (green NVIDIA cube icon). Middle row, left to right: Extract Updates (document with waveform) with a right arrow to Classify and Anonymize (document with magnifier), then a right arrow to NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding (green cube), then a right arrow to Vector DB (database cylinder with lock). Arrows: Sync & Detect Updates down to Extract Updates; Catalog Metadata has a bidirectional vertical arrow down to Classify and Anonymize; Data Governance connects by an elbow line down to Classify and Anonymize; NeMo Retriever Reranking has a bidirectional vertical arrow down to Vector DB; Vector DB has a line right to a vertical line rising to a hexagonal API icon (white outline hexagons with API text). Below a dashed cyan horizontal divider: Access Control (padlock with key icon), Auto Logging (document with waveform and shield icon), Traceability and Versioning (document list icon) with a line right then up to the API icon. Right column: a user silhouette icon (white) with a bidirectional vertical arrow to a blue AI Agent robot icon; NetApp AI Agent (purple circle robot icon) with a bidirectional horizontal arrow to the blue AI Agent; a grey AI Agent robot icon with a bidirectional horizontal arrow from the blue AI Agent; a bidirectional vertical arrow from the blue AI Agent down to NVIDIA Llama Nemotron Reason (green NVIDIA cube). Icons are white line-art except the cyan storage icons, green NVIDIA cubes, purple, blue and grey agent circles. Logos shown: NetApp wordmark logo in top box, NetApp mark on several icons, NVIDIA logo on cubes.

**Design**

Background: Near-black #000000 as rendered, covered by a full-slide image; a #FFFFFF fill lies beneath it. Black background with a thin light grey-to-gold gradient outline frame. Labels are white, sans-serif (close to Helvetica or Inter), regular weight, roughly 1.6% of slide height; the panel title is medium weight, about 2.2%. Accent colours: cyan #1EC8E8 for storage and gear icons, NVIDIA green #76B900, NetApp indigo #1B2A7A for small corner marks, purple #5B4BC4 for the NetApp agent, blue #2E9BF0 for the AI Agent, light grey #C8C8C8 for the second AI agent. Connector lines are thin grey #9A9A9A, about 0.15% stroke. The central panel is dark grey #111111 with a soft corner radius. The dashed divider is cyan #1EC8E8, dashed. Shadows are absent.

**Layout**

A single centred title sits across the top of the slide at about 3% from the top, with a tall left column of category labels and vendor logos and a central content block from about 16% to 92% of the height, aligned to a left edge near 18% and a right edge near 82%. Three stacked bands run down the centre: a row of eight avatars, a dark AIOps panel with seven workflow boxes and logos plus an Orchestrate box, and a block of tan platform bars; the left column carries the Agentic AI, AIOPS and AI Infrastructure labels. There is no footer and no slide number visible.

**Visuals**

Row of eight circular avatar portraits (cartoon 3D renderings of one man with grey hair and glasses in different outfits) in a single horizontal row at about y 16% to 30%: 1 standing in white shirt holding a folder; 2 wearing black headphones and a grey 'Hello World' T-shirt; 3 in a navy blazer with arms crossed; 4 in a white lab coat and goggles holding a green flask; 5 wearing a yellow hard hat and high-visibility vest; 6 in a red patterned jacket; 7 in blue scrubs with a stethoscope holding a pill bottle; 8 in a navy beanie and green plaid shirt. Each avatar is a circular crop with a thin pale rim on a black disc. Lighting is soft studio light; the palette is grey, white, navy, yellow and red. Eight workflow boxes are connected by thin tan horizontal lines: Data Curate, Test and Evaluate, Build, Search, Monitor, Guardrail, Secure, each a rectangle with a thin light-grey outline and sharp corners. Below each box are vendor logos in white: column Data Curate gretel, SuperAnnotate, UNSTRUCTURED; column Test and Evaluate arize, mlflow, Weights & Biases; column Build dataiku, DataRobot, Kubeflow; column Search DATASTAX, elastic, Milvus; column Monitor DATADOG, dynatrace, Grafana Labs; column Guardrail ActiveFence, fiddler, Galileo; column Secure CROWDSTRIKE, securiti, TREND MICRO. A bottom thin tan line runs across under a central Orchestrate box, with logos Canonical, NUTANIX, Red Hat and vmware by Broadcom beneath it. Platform bars: NEMO (wide, left) with a green 3D cube icon overlapping its right end; OMNIVERSE (wide, right) with a green ring icon overlapping its right end; second row CUDA-X, AI-Q, DOCA; third row COMPUTE, STORAGE, NETWORKING. Tan bars have a brushed gold-beige gradient and sharp corners. Left column logos in white: accenture, Deloitte., EY, Infosys, TCS, wipro, and for AI Infrastructure Cisco, DELL Technologies, Hewlett Packard Enterprise, Hitachi Vantara, Lenovo, NetApp. No photographs; the slide is a flat graphic.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-bleed image of black covers the slide beneath the content). Full black background. Title in NVIDIA Sans Medium (a geometric grotesque sans-serif), white #FFFFFF, regular weight, about 3.7% of slide height, centred. Category labels Agentic AI, AIOPS and AI Infrastructure in NVIDIA Sans Regular, tan-gold #C1B485, about 1.7% of height. Workflow box labels in NVIDIA Sans Regular white #FFFFFF about 1.4% of height. Platform bar labels in NVIDIA Sans Medium black #000000 about 1.7% of height. Body text is small and white. Panels use dark grey #111111 with rounded corners about 1% of width. Accents are tan-gold #C1B485 and the green of the NVIDIA icons (#76B900 family). Lines are thin tan-gold, about 1 px. Tan bars have a gradient from light beige #C9B48A to darker #8E7A5A with a subtle sheen and a soft shadow. Slide has no drop shadows on text.

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a single large rounded-outline frame inset about 2 percent from the left and right edges and from the top and bottom, and a boxed CrowdStrike logo centred at the top edge of the frame. No slide title or footer in the conventional sense; the content is a left-to-right flow diagram. Left column holds the SIEM panel above the Training panel; centre holds the Charlotte triage stack above the Data Holdings panel; right holds the Custom Agentic Analysts panel, with the Triage Detection Summary and Human Analyst icons at far right. Top-right carries the Always-on Agentic Analysts label above a stack of three offset agent cards. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Diagram of dark rounded panels connected by grey dashed arrows. Left top panel AI-Powered Falcon Next-Gen SIEM: three text steps Data Ingestion, Data Processing, Data Analytics joined by dashed arrows left to right, then an NVIDIA Morpheus green hexagon icon with a gear below. Arrow from Data Analytics to a warning-triangle icon labelled Threat Detection Documents, then dashed arrow to the Charlotte AI Detection Triage Agent panel: four steps Rule Classifier, Detection Triage, Threat Progression Model, Summarizer joined by dashed arrows; a dashed double-headed arrow runs from Detection Triage down to an NVIDIA NIM green cube icon labelled NVIDIA NIM. Behind the Charlotte panel are three offset dark cards labelled Threat Hunt Agent, Vulnerability Agent and an ellipsis card. Arrow from Summarizer to a document icon labelled Triage Detection Summary, then a solid arrow down to a hat-and-face Human Analyst icon. Bottom left Training panel: gretel wordmark with dashed arrow labelled Synthetic Data pointing to an NVIDIA NeMo green cube; below, an NVIDIA Llama Nemotron Reason green cube with dashed arrow up to the NeMo cube; dashed arrow from Training up to the SIEM panel. Bottom centre Data Holdings panel with red CrowdStrike icon, four outline document icons labelled Digital Twin of Enterprise IT and Cloud, Unique Global Threat Intelligence, CrowdStrike best practices based on 14+ years of global service expertise, and Data mined from customer security events and decisions; solid arrows go up to the Charlotte panel and left to the Training NeMo cube, and right to the Custom Agentic Analysts panel. Custom Agentic Analysts panel holds an NVIDIA NeMo green cube and an NVIDIA DGX Cloud green cloud icon with a grey server. Icons are line-drawn in light grey except the green NVIDIA marks and the red CrowdStrike mark. Always-on Agentic Analysts label has a small circular grey icon of stacked layers.

### Slide 45

**Text**

dataiku / User / Dataiku Platform / Virtual Experimentation Application / Dataiku LLM Guard Services / Dataiku LLM Mesh / NVIDIA LLM NIM / PEFT / Data Flywheel / NVIDIA NeMo Curator / NVIDIA NeMo Customizer / NVIDIA NeMo Evaluator / Curated Datasets / HITL / Information Retrieval / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding

**Layout**

Full-slide architecture diagram with no title. A boxed Dataiku logo is centred at the top edge, overlapping a thin gold frame. Inside the frame, a left-to-right flow runs from User through the Dataiku Platform panel to a large Kubernetes panel on the right, which holds the Data Flywheel panel at upper right and a row of retrieval components along the bottom. The diagram sits on a black field with a wide margin on every side and no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Architecture diagram drawn in white-grey thin line art on black, with NVIDIA green hexagon icons. Shapes: a line-art User figure; a line-art monitor icon labelled Virtual Experimentation Application; a dark grey box labelled Dataiku LLM Guard Services; a small label Dataiku LLM Mesh; a large dark grey rounded panel (Dataiku Platform) containing the User-to-Mesh chain; a blue Kubernetes wheel icon at the top-left of a large dark grey panel; four green hexagon NVIDIA icons (NVIDIA LLM NIM, NVIDIA NeMo Curator, NVIDIA NeMo Customizer, NVIDIA NeMo Evaluator); a dark grey rounded panel labelled Data Flywheel; a green-outlined hexagon gear icon labelled Information Retrieval; two green hexagon icons labelled NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking and NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding; a line-art person icon labelled HITL. Arrows: solid double-headed arrows between User and Virtual Experimentation Application, between the Application and Guard Services, and between Guard Services and LLM Mesh; a solid arrow from LLM Mesh to NVIDIA LLM NIM; a solid arrow labelled PEFT pointing from NeMo Curator's flywheel side to NVIDIA LLM NIM; a solid arrow from NeMo Curator to NeMo Customizer; two solid arrows from NeMo Evaluator up to NeMo Curator and NeMo Customizer; a solid double-headed arrow labelled Curated Datasets from the Flywheel to HITL; a dashed grey elbow line from LLM Mesh down to Information Retrieval; a dashed arrow from NeMo Retriever Reranking to Information Retrieval; a dashed elbow line from NeMo Retriever Embedding up and across to Information Retrieval. Icons are not cropped and the frame is a full-width line.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a white #FFFFFF fill lies beneath, fully covered by a black image).

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a single large rounded-corner frame spanning almost the full width, enclosing a left-to-right architecture diagram. The DataRobot logo box is centred at the top edge of the frame, overlapping its border. Left column holds data sources and ingestion, centre-left holds the Agentic Blueprint panel, centre-right holds Workbench, Agent and bottom Registry and Console panels, and the right column holds the stakeholder teams. No slide title, slide number or footer is present.

**Visuals**

Architecture diagram drawn with flat line icons in blue, white and NVIDIA green on black. Shapes: rounded dark-grey panels labelled Agentic Blueprint (large, left-centre), Workbench (Develop) (top-right), Agent (App Interface) with an inner rounded sub-panel holding Q&A, Alerts and Summary, Registry (Govern) and Console (Monitor) (bottom-right). Icons: inventory table grid, CCTV camera, document stack, cloud with up arrow (Agent Runtime), server stack (Ingestion Workflow), knowledge engineering blocks, NVIDIA green hexagon NIM icons (NVIDIA LLM NIM, NVIDIA NeMo Guardrails as green cube with node network), green Context Manager squares with plus, cylinder database (Graph RAG), hexagon node icons (Vector RAG and Third-party LLM NIM), code-brackets gear (Memory), blocks (Summarization / Alerts Handler), scales (Compare Multiple RAGs), sparkle (Playground), star in circle (LLM Evaluation Metrics), node network (Predictive Forecast Model), code person (Integration Team), monitor (User Interface), bar charts (Groundedness, LLM Cost), shield with check (Prompt Refusal Mitigation), book (Compliance Docs), network nodes (Guard Models), people icons (IT / Ops Team, End User Response Team), person with document (Compliance Officer). Connectors: thin grey dashed lines with small arrowheads route from Data Sources and the Compliance Officer to Workbench, Registry and Console; solid purple (#7F3FBF) lines carry data from Inventory, CCTV and Enterprise Documents to Knowledge Engineering, then to NVIDIA LLM NIM, Context Manager, NeMo Guardrails and the User Interface; solid grey arrows link Vector RAG, Memory, Third-party LLM NIM and Summarization. The DataRobot logo is a white wordmark with a three-bar icon inside a rounded-rectangle box with a light grey border.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 background.

**Layout**

A centred, full-width architecture diagram with no title or footer; a thin gradient-outlined frame runs about 2.3% in from the left and right edges and from the top and bottom, with a DataStax logo box centred on its top edge. Inside, a green-outlined NVIDIA Base Command boundary holds the components: the vector database box top left, the Langflow label left, the RAG Workflow panel centre, the Unified Knowledge Layer panel lower right and the Fine-Tune panel right, with the Arize logo at upper right and a single monitor icon outside the boundary on the left. No slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

Diagram on a black field with no chart or photograph. Shapes: a rounded-corner outlined logo box at top centre containing the DATASTAX wordmark; a dark rounded panel top left labelled DATASTAX with the subtitle NoSQL Vector-enabled Database and three database icons with labels Astra DB (Cloud), HCD (On-prem) and OpenSearch; a green-outlined rectangle labelled NVIDIA Base Command; a dark rounded panel labelled RAG Workflow holding four green hexagonal NVIDIA icons labelled NVIDIA NeMo Retriever, NVIDIA NIM, NVIDIA Blueprints and NVIDIA NeMo Guardrails in a 2 by 2 grid; a dark rounded panel labelled Fine-Tune holding two green hexagonal icons labelled NVIDIA NeMo Customizer and NVIDIA NeMo Evaluator; a dark rounded panel labelled Unified Knowledge Layer with the DATASTAX wordmark and a bullet list of five items. Logos: DATASTAX (top box, top-left panel and knowledge panel), Langflow (icon and wordmark), arize (magenta caret mark and wordmark), OpenSearch, Astra DB, HCD icons, NVIDIA hexagon icons. Connectors: a grey vertical line from the top edge of the boundary down to the Unified Knowledge Layer, labelled Enterprise Messages, with an arrowhead pointing down at the panel; a solid grey arrow from Langflow pointing right into RAG Workflow; a dashed grey arrow from Fine-Tune pointing left into RAG Workflow; a grey vertical double-headed arrow from Langflow down to the App and 3rd Party API icon; a grey elbow line from below Langflow down and right with an arrowhead into the Unified Knowledge Layer, with an upward arrowhead at the Langflow end; a grey double-headed horizontal arrow from the App icon to the Unified Knowledge Layer; a grey line from the Unified Knowledge Layer right edge out to the right, up and left with an arrowhead into arize; a short grey vertical line from arize down to Fine-Tune; a grey arrow from the Unified Knowledge Layer bottom downward labelled Messages; a grey double-headed arrow between the monitor icon and the boundary. Other icons: a white monitor icon at left and a white code-brackets icon labelled App and 3rd Party API Services.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000, with a full-slide white #FFFFFF fill beneath it. Black background, white titles and body text, NVIDIA green #76B900 for the boundary outline and hexagon icons, dark panels #111111 with rounded corners of about 1% of slide width, grey #9A9A9A connectors, magenta #E8336B for the arize logo. Typefaces: the DATASTAX wordmark is a wide geometric sans; labels are a regular sans-serif such as Helvetica or Arial; panel headings are a bold sans-serif. Title text about 1.8% of slide height; body and labels about 1.2% to 1.5%. Shadows: none. Gradient: the outer frame line is a faint gradient from grey to warm tones.

**Layout**

A centred technical architecture diagram with no title, footer or slide number. A thin rounded outline frame spans most of the slide width, with the Elastic logo box centred on its top edge. Ingestion runs in the top band and Retrieval in the lower band, separated by a dashed horizontal divider; the lower band flows right to left, with a bottom row that loops back to the right-hand database panel.

**Visuals**

Diagram on black. Shapes and labels, left to right and top to bottom: an orange square icon (#E8642C) labelled 'Multimodal Enterprise Data' with a right-pointing arrow to a green NVIDIA NeMo hexagon icon labelled 'NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Extraction', which has a right-pointing arrow to a second green hexagon labelled 'NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding'. A vertical arrow runs down from that hexagon to the top edge of the Elasticsearch Vector Database panel. In the Retrieval row, a gold group-of-people icon (#D4A017) on the far left has a left-pointing arrow labelled 'Response' to a green hexagon 'NVIDIA NeMo Guardrails', which has a left arrow from a green hexagon 'LLM' (the LLM hexagon has a left arrow from the reranking hexagon). The reranking hexagon labelled 'NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking' receives a left-pointing arrow from the Elasticsearch panel on the right. The Elasticsearch Vector Database panel is a dark rounded rectangle (#161616) containing the Elasticsearch logo (yellow, white and teal mark), the text 'Elasticsearch Vector Database', the bold label 'NVIDIA cuVS', and two icons: a yellow magnifier labelled 'Search (CPU & GPU)' and a yellow gear labelled 'Indexing (GPU)', each with a green underline bar. From the people icon a vertical line with a 'Query' label on a dark tile runs down, then turns right with an arrow to a second green hexagon 'NVIDIA NeMo Guardrails', which has a right arrow to a green hexagon 'NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding'; a line from that hexagon runs right then up with an arrowhead into the bottom edge of the Elasticsearch panel. Connectors are thin grey (#8A8A8A) lines, about 1 px, with small triangular arrowheads. Elastic logo: multicolour dots (yellow, blue, pink, teal, green) beside a white lowercase 'elastic' wordmark.

**Layout**

Centered single-column architecture diagram on a black field. The Nutanix wordmark box sits at top centre, overlapping a thin rounded outer frame that spans nearly the full slide with about 2% margins. Inside the frame, a large dark panel labelled Nutanix Enterprise AI occupies the centre-right, with an AI User and RAG App / Agent column at far left outside it, and a Nutanix Kubernetes Platform bar along the bottom of the panel. No footer, source line or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Architecture diagram with no photographs. Shapes: a large dark grey rounded panel (Nutanix Enterprise AI) containing four top icons (Role Based Access Controls: stacked grey card/tray icon; Day 2 Operations: purple capsule with grey cursor; Enterprise LLM Repository: grid of white cubes around a purple chip), a nested darker panel titled Inference Endpoint Service containing four green NVIDIA hexagon icons labelled NVIDIA NeMo Guardrails, NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking, NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding and NVIDIA Llama Nemotron Reason, a grey dashed horizontal divider, and a row with RAG Inference Endpoint Service (bold) followed by four white/purple cube-cluster icons labelled Guardrail Endpoint, Embedding Endpoint, Reranking Endpoint and Reasoning Endpoint. Left column: AI User icon (monitor with purple person) connected by a vertical double-headed thin grey arrow to a RAG App / Agent icon (purple robot), which connects by a horizontal double-headed thin grey arrow to the Inference Endpoint Service row. Bottom bar contains two green hexagon gear icons labelled NVIDIA GPU Operator and NVIDIA Network Operator, with the text Nutanix Kubernetes Platform (NKP) between them. Top-centre logo is the white NUTANIX wordmark inside a thin light-grey rounded rectangle outline.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black background with a thin pale gold-to-grey gradient rounded outline frame. Titles and labels white #FFFFFF, body labels light grey #D0D0D0, accents NVIDIA green #76B900 and purple #7B5CF0. Typeface for labels is a light geometric sans-serif (Helvetica-like), inside-panel heading in bold sans-serif. Title text small relative to slide height (about 1.8% for headings, about 1.4% for labels). Panels are dark grey #111111 and #1A1A1A with rounded corners of about 1% of slide width, no visible shadows. Dashed divider in grey #6B6B6B.

**Layout**

A centred Red Hat logo box sits at the top of a thin-outlined frame that spans most of the slide. Inside the frame, three stacked dark panels run full width of the content area: a large top panel with the OpenShift AI diagram, a middle band holding three NVIDIA operator icons in a row, and a slim bottom band with the OpenShift logo and six platform items. No slide title, footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Diagram of a stacked architecture. Top panel: Red Hat OpenShift AI logo at top left; NVIDIA NIM hexagonal green 3D cube icon at top centre above the label NVIDIA NIM; left spinner ring icon labelled Agentic App Development; three rounded rectangles in a row (TensorRT-LLM outlined in bright green, vLLM, sGLang) above a wide rounded rectangle labelled Optimized Profiles; two small arrows (one pointing down, one pointing up) below it leading to the bold label Model Monitoring & Observability; a row of five white line icons (Metrics bar-chart monitor, Telemetry gauge dial, Accelerator Profiles chip, Logging document, Alerting warning triangle) each with a label; right spinner ring icon labelled Prompts and Responses. Middle band: three green hexagon gear icons labelled NVIDIA GPU Operator, NVIDIA Network Operator, NVIDIA NIM Operator. Bottom band: Red Hat OpenShift logo and white line icons labelled Enterprise Kubernetes (cluster nodes), Enterprise Linux (terminal with cursor), Container & VM Management (hexagon and chip), DevOps Tooling (infinity loop), Security (fingerprint), Ecosystem (network node cluster). Spinner rings are grey partial arcs with a small arrowhead. Lighting is flat; no photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with the full-slide picture behind everything. Near-black background. Panels are very dark grey #111111 with subtle rounded corners and no shadows. Titles none. Panel text is white #FFFFFF or light grey #E6E6E6 in a clean sans-serif like Red Hat Text, regular weight, about 1.2 percent of slide height for labels and 1.5 percent for the bold Model Monitoring label. Accents are Red Hat red #EE0000 for the hat logos and NVIDIA green #76B900 for the hexagon icons and the TensorRT-LLM outline. Frame outline is a thin light grey to gold gradient line, about 0.1 percent of slide height. Top logo box is black with a thin light border and a soft shadow.

**Layout**

A full-slide black frame holds a centred Trend Micro logo badge at the top and three rounded dark panels below it: a wide main panel (Trend Vision One™, two process rows plus a shared pipeline) above two smaller panels side by side (Vulnerability Management on the left, Training on the right). Panel titles sit at the top-left of each panel in bold, left-aligned; the content is laid out left to right in rows with arrows between nodes; there is no footer, slide number or date.

**Visuals**

Diagram with no chart or photograph. Row 1 of the main panel (Extended Detection and Response XDR): a red-outlined Input document icon, arrow right to a green hexagon gear icon labelled NVIDIA Morpheus, arrow right to a grey funnel icon with a small red dot labelled Sweeping Logs For Correlation, arrow right to a green hexagon network-node icon labelled NVIDIA NIM Investigation Agent, arrow right to a grey outline warning triangle with red exclamation mark labelled Events Alerts. Row 2 (Data Security Posture Management): red-outlined Input icon, arrow right to green hexagon gear icon labelled NVIDIA RAPIDS cuDF, arrow right to a small dark grey rectangle with tiny white text Trend Micro Data Classification Engine labelled Parallel Rules Processing, arrow right to green hexagon network-node icon labelled NVIDIA NIM Classification Agent, arrow right to outline warning triangle with red exclamation mark labelled Detection Alerts. A dashed grey line starts below Input (row 2) and runs down and right to a green hexagon gear icon labelled NVIDIA Morpheus Data Pipeline, then a dashed line runs from that icon up to the right end under Detection Alerts, forming a loop. Vulnerability Management panel: grey outline user icon with red base labelled User, arrow right to green hexagon network-node icon labelled NVIDIA NIM Trend Cybertron, arrow right labelled Inspection, leading to a grey outline cloud with a small shield icon labelled Code Repos Containers Kubernetes Cloud Resources, with the text Inspection Result: Misconfigurations /CVEs above the arrow area. Training panel: three grey outline cylinder database icons labelled Threat Intelligence Repository, Trend Vision One™ Data Lake, and a grey wrench icon with red motion lines labelled Remediation Actions; their lines join into a grey bracket that drops to a centre point, then a dark green hexagon with a grey tree-diagram icon labelled NVIDIA NeMo, arrow right to a green hexagon network-node icon labelled NVIDIA NIM Trend Cybertron. Logo: Trend Micro logo in the top-centre badge, a red circle with a white swoosh and the white wordmark TREND with small MICRO and ™. Dominant colours are NVIDIA green #76B900 with black and white line icons and red #D0021B accents. Flat vector style, no photographs, no cropping, no gradient fills.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-slide background image behind everything, with a white #FFFFFF fill underneath that is fully covered. Black background, flat dark-grey rounded panels with no shadows. Panel fill #111111 with a faint 1px grey #2A2A2A edge; outer frame is a thin hairline in a bronze to grey gradient stroke. Titles are white #FFFFFF bold sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans-like) at about 2.0 percent of slide height. Body labels are light grey #E6E6E6 regular sans-serif at about 1.4 percent of slide height. Accents: NVIDIA green #76B900 hexagons, red #D0021B icon accents, dashed grey #8C8C8C connector lines, arrows in grey #9A9A9A with about 1px stroke. Logo badge has a thin silver #C8C8C8 rounded outline with about 1 percent corner radius. Panel corner radius small, about 0.5 percent of slide width.

**Layout**

A single bordered, rounded frame fills almost the whole slide, with the Weights & Biases logo box centred at the top edge of the frame. The upper half is a left-to-right flow of the inference workflow with a dashed horizontal divider separating it from the lower training workflow, which has two dark rounded panels side by side (left panel Automated Dataset Curation, right panel Automated Customize-Evaluate-Promote Loop) with the W&B Artifacts icon between them. Left-aligned workflow labels sit at x about 9.5%, no title placeholder, no footer, no page number.

**Visuals**

Diagram (flowchart) on black. Shapes: NVIDIA NeMo cube icons (green lattice cubes, 7 total: NeMo Guardrails, NeMo Retriever, NVIDIA NIM, NeMo (in dataset panel), NeMo Curator, NeMo Customizer, NeMo Evaluator); yellow rounded-square icons (User person icon, Front End screen icon, Plan branch icon, Enterprise Data database icon, W&B Traces chat icon is grey-circle, Raw Feedback Database, Human Labeling, Customer Processing, Cleaned Feedback Database, Promote Models wand); grey circle icons (W&B Guardrails hazard icon, W&B Feedback chat icon, W&B Traces chat icon, W&B Artifacts layers icon, W&B Experiments flask, W&B Evaluations bar-chart icon, W&B Registry books icon). Arrows: thin grey 1 px lines with small arrowheads. User to NeMo Guardrails (leftward arrow); NeMo Guardrails up to W&B Guardrails; NeMo Guardrails right to Front End; Front End double-headed to Plan; Plan double-headed to NVIDIA NIM; Plan up to NeMo Retriever; Enterprise Data left to NeMo Retriever; NeMo Retriever right and down to W&B Traces; NVIDIA NIM right to W&B Traces; Front End down, then left to W&B Feedback labelled Implicit Feedback; W&B Feedback left to User labelled Explicit Feedback (line goes up at x about 24%); W&B Feedback down to Raw Feedback Database and down to NeMo lines; NVIDIA NIM down-left to NIM Logs line and to W&B Feedback; NIM Logs line runs from left panel to NVIDIA NIM; Updated Model line from NVIDIA NIM right then down to Promote Models. Inside left panel: Raw Feedback Database, Human Labeling, NVIDIA NeMo, Customer Processing feed down into NVIDIA NeMo Curator via bracket lines, Curator right to Cleaned Feedback Database, which right to W&B Artifacts, which right to W&B Experiments. Inside right panel: NeMo Customizer double-headed with a circled X between it and NeMo Evaluator; NeMo Evaluator right to a circled check mark then Promote Models; Customizer down to W&B Experiments, Evaluator down to W&B Evaluations, Promote Models down to W&B Registry. Dashed horizontal divider in light grey #8A8A8A, 1 px dashes, at y about 49%, spanning x 9.25% to 91%. Logo: W&B Weights & Biases mark (small orange dot-grid symbol) with serif wordmark, inside a rounded rectangle with a cream outline.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (full-slide image beneath the diagram, with a white #FFFFFF fill underneath it). Dark, technical diagram style. Background black #000000. Titles and panel headings white #FFFFFF, semibold sans-serif, about 1.6% of slide height. Body labels off-white #E6E6E6 regular sans-serif about 1.2% of slide height; secondary labels (Online Processing, Offline Processing) grey #9A9A9A. Accent colours: NVIDIA green #76B900 with darker green #3F6B00 shading on the cubes; W&B icon yellow #F5B731 on yellow tiles; grey icon circles #2A2A2A. Logo wordmark in cream-white serif (close to Garamond-like serif), about 2.4% of slide height, subtitle 'by CoreWeave' in small sans-serif. Connectors and arrows light grey #B0B0B0, 1 px stroke. Panels fill #111111 with corner radius about 1.5% of slide width. Outer frame: thin cream-to-brown gradient line about 1 px with rounded corners, no fill. No shadows.

**Layout**

Title is centred across the top at about 3% from the top edge, spanning most of the slide width. Three zones sit below it: a left column of white-logo category labels with logo clusters (Agentic AI, AIOPS, AI Infrastructure) at x about 3 to 16%, a central column of two dark rounded panels (avatar strip at y 15.4 to 30.7%, AIOPS platform panel at y 35.3 to 67.3%) spanning x 18.3 to 81.8%, and a stack of tan platform bars below (y 73 to 91%). No footer, slide number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Eight circular-cropped 3D cartoon avatars of one man with grey hair and glasses in different outfits, in a horizontal row inside the top dark panel. Left to right: in a white shirt holding a folder; in a black t-shirt with headphones reading 'Hello World'; in a navy blazer with arms crossed; in a white lab coat holding a green flask; in a yellow hard hat and high-visibility vest; in a red patterned jacket; in a blue scrub top holding a clipboard and a small device; in a green plaid shirt and dark beanie. Each avatar sits on a black circle with a thin gold-grey ring. The AIOPS panel holds an 8-column pipeline of outlined boxes labelled Data Curate, Test and Evaluate, Build, Search, Monitor, Guardrail and Secure, joined by thin grey horizontal lines, with three rows of partner logos under each box and a centre box Orchestrate below with a thin gold horizontal line running across the panel. Top-right of the platform: a green NVIDIA NeMo cube icon overlapping the NEMO bar and a green ring icon overlapping the OMNIVERSE bar. Logo clusters are white monochrome logos on black. Lower platform stack: NEMO and OMNIVERSE wide bars over three rows of CUDA-X, AI-Q, DOCA and COMPUTE, STORAGE, NETWORKING bars in a tan gradient.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed (a white #FFFFFF fill lies beneath the full-slide image).

### Slide 54

**Text**

Disney Research / NVIDIA / Google DeepMind / Newton / MuJoCo Solver / Disney Research Solver / Custom Solver / NVIDIA Warp / MuJoCo Playground / Other Simulator / Isaac Lab

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with three logos in a single row across the top (about 12% to 17% from the top). Below, a large rounded-corner solver diagram panel sits on the left (x 6.3 to 43.4%, y 28.7 to 80.7%), a small centre column holds MuJoCo Playground and Other Simulator, and a large rounded photo sits on the right (x 56.8 to 93.9%, y 28.7 to 80.7%) with the caption 'Isaac Lab' centred beneath it. There is no title, no footer and no slide number; the content is roughly centred horizontally with wide side margins.

**Visuals**

Diagram with a rounded black panel containing a flow of solver icons. Top: a yellow-outlined Newton icon (a wireframe cube with grid) with green underline, labelled Newton, with a vertical arrow pointing up into it from the centre solver. Middle row: three yellow gear icons with green underlines, from left to right labelled MuJoCo Solver, Disney Research Solver and Custom Solver. A grey bracket line joins the top of the MuJoCo Solver and Custom Solver icons, rising to a horizontal segment at about 46% height that meets a vertical arrow from the Disney Research Solver up to Newton. A second grey bracket joins the bottom of the MuJoCo Solver and Custom Solver icons and meets a vertical arrow from the Disney Research Solver down to the NVIDIA Warp icon. Bottom: a yellow gear icon with green underline labelled NVIDIA Warp. Centre column: a small dark-blue rectangle with a grid pattern and a grey humanoid robot thumbnail, labelled MuJoCo Playground, and below it a small green-outlined icon with four squares and a plus sign, labelled Other Simulator. A double-headed arrow connects the diagram panel's right edge to the MuJoCo Playground box. A grey line runs down from the Newton-side connector, branches right to an arrowhead at the Isaac Lab photo, and branches to an arrowhead at Other Simulator. Photo: a full-colour render of a grey-white wheeled and legged robot with a flat rectangular head carrying three lenses and an antenna, standing on a rocky desert of sand and pebbles under a pale blue sky with brown mesas on the horizon. Warm sunlight from the left, dominant colours tan, ochre, pale blue and grey-white. The photo is not cropped, sits inside a rounded-corner box with a thin grey border, and has no overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 background. Solid black background. Logos and labels in white #FFFFFF. Accent colour NVIDIA green #76B900 for the underlines under icons and the Other Simulator icon outline. Gear icons are yellow-orange #F2B705 with dark outlines. Connector and panel strokes are thin mid-grey #8C8C8C, about 1 px. Typeface is a clean sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans style, close to Helvetica or Arial) in regular weight; labels are small, about 1.5% of slide height for diagram labels and about 1.4% for the caption. Logos are the Disney Research script wordmark, the NVIDIA eye logo with wordmark and the Google DeepMind swirl with wordmark, all in white. The diagram panel has corner radius about 2% of slide width and no shadow; the photo has similar rounded corners.

Centred title block at the top, with the headline in gold on line one and the white subtitle on line two, both centred on the slide. Below, a single horizontal row runs left to right: a rounded video frame with a caption box beneath it, two small green NVIDIA-style icons with labels joined by thin arrows, and a second rounded video frame. Dim humanoid robot figures flank the left and right edges, and there is no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Four elements. (1) Left rounded video still, about 22.7 by 22.7 percent of the slide, showing a robot lab with two dexterous black robot hands over a light wood table. On the table are a white spray bottle with a red trigger, a white cylindrical cup, a black round object and a purple bin, with a small image placeholder icon centred in the frame. The frame is full colour, not cropped, with a thin light-grey border and rounded corners. (2) Two green, faceted, cube-like NVIDIA-style icons, each about 5 by 9 percent, with white labels below: NVIDIA Cosmos (left) and Action Labeling (right). (3) Three thin grey arrows, each a plain line with a small arrowhead pointing right, connecting the left frame to Cosmos, Cosmos to Action Labeling, and Action Labeling to the right frame. (4) Right rounded video still of the same lab, about 22.8 percent square, showing the same table with the robot hand now holding a grey arm reaching toward the black object, with coloured motion trails in magenta, cyan and green. A translucent white play button in a square outline is centred on the frame. Both frames have a dark grey 1 px border and about 1.5 percent corner radius. Dim, low-key, desaturated humanoid robot figures appear at the left and right edges, partly cropped by the slide edges, in charcoal grey with a blue ring light on the right figure's head. A caption box sits under the left frame: a rectangle with a thin light-grey outline, about 0.15 percent stroke, small corner radius, transparent fill, containing the white text described below.

Background: Solid black #000000 with dim robot figures in charcoal at the left and right edges. Pure black background with a dark, cinematic look. One photograph of a humanoid robot standing behind a light wooden workbench. In front of it sits a raised pale wooden block on a wooden table top, with a yellow base plate under the robot's feet. Behind it is a warehouse with grey wire-mesh walls, black metal shelving, orange plastic storage bins with white label strips on the left and right, yellow bins on the upper shelves, a blue sign on the left wall, and a small yellow bin on the left shelf. Composition: eye-level, frontal, centred, full-frame within the slide, not cropped at the subject. Lighting: even overhead fluorescent light, neutral and soft. Dominant colours: grey and silver for the robot, white hard hat, orange and yellow bins, light beige wall, pale wood. The photograph is boxed with rounded corners and is not full-bleed. No overlay or tint is visible.

### Slide 60

**Text**

Omniverse Digital Twin / wiwynn / Smart Manufacturing / MFG AI / Automation / Digital Twin / AI-AOI/AXI Inspection / Defect Detection / Fail Analysis & Suggestion / Layout Optimization / Product Simulation / Cobot Integration / NVIDIA GPU Computing / Model Training & Inference / Data Ingestion / LLM + RAG / NVIDIA Omniverse / Isaac Simulation / Path Planning & Environment / USD-based Simulation and Visualization Pipeline / Realtime Collaboration / 30001

**Layout**

Full-bleed 3D rendered photograph covering the whole slide with no margins and no visible title placeholder. The bottom-left caption label 'Omniverse Digital Twin' sits flush to the left edge at about 90 to 97 percent height, and a dark rounded information panel sits at the lower right, inset about 2 percent from the right edge and about 3.7 percent from the bottom edge. No footer, slide number or date is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photorealistic 3D render of a smart electronics assembly line viewed from a high oblique angle. In the foreground centre, a white SCARA robot with blue joint covers and a silver vertical ram and tool head is placing components onto a green-topped conveyor pallet holding a green server or motherboard assembly. Left of centre, a stack of two grey AGV-style trolleys carries black plastic bins with white inserts holding green PCB pieces, and the lower trolley shows the label '30001' in dark blue. Behind, a second robot with a blue top sits on a grey machine at upper left, and a further assembly station with green PCB trays sits at upper right. Green-topped conveyor sections run horizontally across the image, with orange and red status lights and orange emergency stop buttons along the rails. At right, stacked white trays sit on a grey conveyor. Lighting is soft, diffuse and even with grey and white surfaces. Dominant colours are white, light grey, silver, mint green #7FD9A0 and blue #1F5FC0. The photograph is not cropped and is not boxed. An overlay panel covers the lower right. The dark panel shows a Smart Manufacturing flow diagram: a central grey header box with three green-outlined circular icons labelled MFG AI, Automation and Digital Twin. Left column boxes labelled AI-AOI/AXI Inspection, Defect Detection and Fail Analysis & Suggestion connect by thin grey lines with arrowheads into the central header. Right column boxes labelled Layout Optimization, Product Simulation and Cobot Integration connect from the header by thin grey lines with arrowheads. Two bottom cards: NVIDIA GPU Computing (listing Model Training & Inference, Data Ingestion, LLM + RAG) with a grey outline, and NVIDIA Omniverse (listing Isaac Simulation, Path Planning & Environment, USD-based Simulation and Visualization Pipeline, Realtime Collaboration) with a bright green outline, and a green ring NVIDIA Omniverse icon above it. The wiwynn logo sits on a white rounded tab at the top edge of the panel.

**Design**

Background: Photograph of the smart manufacturing line filling the whole slide, with a light grey and white backdrop; the base fill is #FFFFFF.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph fills the whole slide. A white caption label sits at the bottom left, edge to edge with the slide margin at x 0. A dark rounded-corner panel sits at the lower right, with a gold-outlined PEGATRON badge overlapping its top edge. No visible title placeholder, footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Photograph of an industrial factory floor rendered as a 3D digital twin. Subject: a long row of grey and white automated machines and conveyor modules on a glossy white floor, with yellow-and-black hazard lines and a red and green signal tower in the foreground. Red-uniformed and blue-uniformed workers are small in the background left. Shelving racks on the right hold dark boxes, and a grey automated guided vehicle carrying stacked black trays sits at right. Two wall-mounted dashboard screens with gauges and charts appear at the top centre. Composition is a one-point perspective shot from the front-left, full-bleed, uncropped at edges. Lighting is soft, even overhead daylight with bright white reflections on the floor. Dominant colours are white, light grey, charcoal and black, with small red and green accents. Overlay: a dark inset panel titled PEGATRON AI Factory with three columns of icons (World Model, AI Agent, Data), three product boxes (PEGAVERSE, PEGA AI, PEGATRON IoT) and a row of three server images labelled Omniverse Enterprise, AI Enterprise and IGX, plus a column titled AI-Powered Factory with two photos (Virtual World, Real World) and a row of three hardware images, joined by arrows labelled Intelligence and Data and a central line labelled Digital Twin Synchronization. Panel is black with a thin gold border, and the top-level tiles use green #76B900 header labels.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of a white and grey factory floor. Photographic slide with a white caption label and a black inset panel. Background: photograph (white and grey factory). Title colour: black #000000 for the caption text. Body text colour inside panel: white #FFFFFF and pale grey #C8C8C8 for small labels. Accent colour: NVIDIA green #76B900 for the caption bar and tile headers, gold #C9A24A for the PEGATRON badge border and text. Typeface: NVIDIA Sans Bold, sans-serif, caption text about 2.2% of slide height. Panel labels are tiny sans-serif in regular weight. Caption box has no corner radius and no shadow. The inset panel has rounded corners of about 1% of slide width and a faint shadow.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a futuristic factory floor rendered as a digital twin. In the foreground a single grey humanoid robot stands centred with its back to the camera, facing a long row of aluminium-frame trolleys with flat grey shelves. Small black electronic components sit on the trolley shelves at left and right of the robot. Behind it, rows of identical grey humanoid robots stand at workstations along both sides of a central aisle, receding toward the back of the hall. Composition is a deep one-point perspective with the central aisle as the vanishing line. Lighting is soft, diffuse and overhead, with reflections on the polished grey floor. Dominant colours are light grey, silver and white with black accents. The photograph is full-bleed, not cropped. An inset tablet mockup shows a black rounded-rectangle screen with a thin gold-brown bezel, containing a dashboard of five columns: Industry (four red circular icons with labels Electronic Information, Automotive and Parts, Household Appliance, New Energy); SaaS (a 2-by-4 grid of small blue, green and red screenshot thumbnails under Digitalization and Intelligence); PaaS (a green-headed NVIDIA Omniverse Platform panel with six green-icon tiles, a General Intelligence Large Model panel, a Third-Party Large Models panel and a Platform Tools panel with small icons); IoT (a Robotization panel with four small robot-arm images and an Automation panel with four factory thumbnails). The Foxconn logo is a white badge with a red H mark and white FOXCONN wordmark on a black rounded rectangle. No charts are present.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a GIGABYTE SMT production line interior. In the centre is a white GIGABYTE GPX-C machine with a black glass front panel, a red emergency stop button on its left upper housing, and a green-and-white status panel. To its left is a white cabinet with a small dark display and a red button. To the right is a monitor on an articulated arm with a keyboard and mouse. Behind are rows of white equipment with traffic-light towers showing red and green, and a teal-framed doorway at the far left. The floor is green and the walls are white; the lighting is even and bright, with overhead fixtures visible at the top. The camera looks slightly down from the front, framed as a wide shot. A dark dashboard overlay in the lower right, about 32% wide, shows a flow diagram: a Decision Maker icon (blue circle) on the left, a Cloud box with a 3D view thumbnail, Front End (Tailwind, React JS), Omniverse App Streaming node (green circle) connected by arrows to 3D Viewport, Omniverse Database and File Database, and a Process Maintenance Data and Retriever pair in a RAG Extension box with NVIDIA LLM NIM and Langchain nodes feeding Omniverse Extension. A lower SDG box holds Training Model, Image Database and NVIDIA Replicator nodes, with Omniverse Nucleus below the Cloud box. An Edge box holds a production-line machine image, SMT Reflow Oven, AOI and SPI icons, and an IOC box linked to Omni USD. Industry Robotic Process Automation with IsaacLab sits at the bottom of Cloud. Arrows are thin grey lines with small arrowheads. The overlay is a boxed screen capture with a thin gold-grey border and a rounded corner radius. The photograph is not cropped.

Full-bleed photograph of four identical automated assembly cells in a bright white factory hall, shown in a straight-on, slightly elevated wide frame. Each cell has a white overhead cabinet, aluminium extrusion frame, a blue collaborative robot arm with a white gripper picking server boards, dark green fixture boards with copper-finish server components, a keyboard and mouse on a drawer, and green status lights on tower beacons. Lighting is even overhead fluorescent with soft shadows. Dominant colours are white, light grey, aluminium silver, blue robot arms and dark green boards. The photo is not cropped. Overlaid on the lower right is a boxed dark diagram panel (about black #0A0A0A, thin dark green border) with a Quanta Computer logo box above it, showing: a left column with a SIEMENS box (Siemens Teamcenter and Siemens Teamcenter Process & Plant Simulation) and a DELMIA box (3DEXPERIENCE Delmia); arrows pointing left from the Quanta and Omniverse boxes to these; a Quanta server box labelled QuantaGrid D52G-2U3 GPU RTX A6000 (about) with a server illustration; a central green-outlined box labelled NVIDIA Omniverse with the NVIDIA logo; a Quanta server box labelled QuantaGrid D75E-4U GPU L40S (about) with a server illustration; and a right column of four NVIDIA-green-icon boxes labelled Isaac Sim, USD Composer, Replicator and Blender, joined by thin grey arrows from the Omniverse box. The Quanta Computer logo (red and blue Q mark with Chinese characters and Quanta Computer text) sits on a black rounded tab with thin grey outline. The label box is white with a bold black sans-serif caption and a green bar beneath.

A red emergency-stop button sits on the right edge. Lower on the plate are dark grey clamp blocks with holes.
